## 2026-06-09 strategy / PMO / architecture / roadmap chain (DR-0017–DR-0049)

These records came from the 2026-06-09 documentation chain (PoeTech market strategy + Legal Module, the PMO + Industry/Role Module Template, Architecture Principles, and the Build Roadmap). They were originally numbered DR-001… in a separate three-digit lineage and were **renumbered +16 to continue this ledger’s single sequential numbering on landing (2026-06-09)** — e.g. former DR-001 → DR-0017. They retain their source schema (Owner + Source doc) because they reference Layer-4 source docs rather than per-DR files; normalizing them into the Tier/Entities/Grounds columns above is a later, optional pass.

| # | Date | Decision (one line) | Owner | Status | Source doc |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **DR-0017** | 2026-06-09 | Build the **Workforce Layer** (guided tasks + LLM QA gate + 1099 payouts) as PoeTech's delivery engine and market centerpiece — encoding Darrell's expertise so the hiring bar becomes capacity, not credentials. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-poetech-market-strategy-workforce-three-ring.md` |
| **DR-0018** | 2026-06-09 | Pursue a **three-ring market** — Ring 1 COLG (gift/reference), Ring 2 other churches (productized sovereign node / managed IT), Ring 3 businesses (paid tiers) — with **paid rings subsidizing the free mission tier.** | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-poetech-market-strategy-workforce-three-ring.md` |
| **DR-0019** | 2026-06-09 | **Do NOT build a money rail.** Integrate an established contractor-payments platform (payouts, W-9, 1099-NEC, milestone/escrow) as an accepted, swappable vendor dependency; LLMs orchestrate logic, platform owns compliance. | Darrell (vendor) + platform (compliance) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-poetech-market-strategy-workforce-three-ring.md` |
| **DR-0020** | 2026-06-09 | **Quality is gated by the LLM Quality Gatekeeper** against explicit per-task acceptance criteria; payout follows a QA pass; rework loop; reputation compounds; high-stakes escalates behind the Cage; **no rubber-stamping.** | Darrell (bar) + Gatekeeper (exec) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-poetech-market-strategy-workforce-three-ring.md` |
| **DR-0021** | 2026-06-09 | **Worker classification (1099 vs employee) routes to an employment/labor attorney, NOT to Trevor (real-estate).** Start clearly-1099; Illinois has its own rules. *(Not legal advice.)* | Employment attorney (owns) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-poetech-market-strategy-workforce-three-ring.md` |
| **DR-0022** | 2026-06-09 | The **first concrete Ring-3 tenant is a law firm running its practice on PoeTech**, which also serves **PoeTech's own legal needs (dogfood).** | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-legal-module-spec.md` |
| **DR-0023** | 2026-06-09 | Legal Module **confidentiality = HIGHEST tier** (privilege treated like the TLC HIPAA tier: per-client + per-matter segregation + conflict checks); **sovereignty is the selling point** (client data never leaves the firm's control — a claim no cloud SaaS can match). | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-legal-module-spec.md` |
| **DR-0024** | 2026-06-09 | Legal Module **LLM assist behind heavy guardrails:** lawyer owns all advice/output; no unauthorized practice of law; LLM never advises clients directly; mandatory human review; no client data crosses matters/clients. | Darrell (governs) + reviewing attorney | Accepted | `2026-06-09-legal-module-spec.md` |
| **DR-0025** | 2026-06-09 | Build a **reusable SKOS "law firm" role-module template** (config-driven; legal-specific extensions written once) so the next firm onboards in days. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-legal-module-spec.md` |
| **DR-0026** | 2026-06-09 | Route the **real-estate / Poe Properties flows** and the **contractor-agreement form** to Trevor (real-estate counsel); **explicitly exclude worker classification** from his scope; request a referral to employment counsel. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-trevor-real-estate-lawyer-review-package.md` |
| **DR-0027** | 2026-06-09 | Build a **sovereign, LLM-native PMO / Project-Portfolio Module** as **instance #1 of the reusable Industry/Role Module pattern**; its **canonical output is a living portfolio overview** (mirrors the DR framework + project-review format) — not a static write-up. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-poetech-pmo-portfolio-module-spec.md` |
| **DR-0028** | 2026-06-09 | The PMO module **federates existing systems** (Monday/GitHub Projects, GitHub, n8n, calendar, DR ledger, events-as-data/observability, 1099 workforce, QA Gatekeeper wf36, identity, Cage, four-entity roll-up); **the ONLY net-new component is the thin LLM synthesis/orchestration layer** (ingest → synthesize → route). | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-poetech-pmo-portfolio-module-spec.md` |
| **DR-0029** | 2026-06-09 | **PM-as-automation:** the PMO module enables a **non-PM to manage at PM quality** via **GUIDE + ENFORCE + ESCALATE**; the **Cage governs autonomous re-sequencing** (safe re-plans autonomous, risky changes human-gated). Same "replaces the network engineer" economics; ships as a prebuilt SKOS "Project Manager" role module. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-poetech-pmo-portfolio-module-spec.md` |
| **DR-0030** | 2026-06-09 | Abstract a **reusable Industry/Role Module Template** (five-part blueprint: encode the discipline / federate existing systems / plug into the shared OS spine / feed the pipeline / onboard by configuration) so **every value-adding industry plugs in by configuration, not custom build** ("new industry in days"). | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-industry-role-module-template-spec.md` |
| **DR-0031** | 2026-06-09 | **Each module = a tenant + a market segment + a workforce category + a data source**; rank candidate industries by **pipeline value** tied to the three-ring market + the Workforce Layer (prefer modules that productize work already delivered by hand and that we can dogfood). | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-industry-role-module-template-spec.md` |
| **DR-0032** | 2026-06-09 | Recorded **instances** of the pattern — **#1 PMO**, **#2 Legal** — plus a prioritized **candidate-module list** (IT/network ops, accounting/bookkeeping, HR/recruiting, marketing, real-estate/property mgmt, church operations, counseling [TLC — ISO-1 walled, sovereign-only]). | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-industry-role-module-template-spec.md` |
| **DR-0033** | 2026-06-09 | Adopt **the Want-To-Use Bar** as the senior success metric for every role module: PoeTech must be so good the role-holder **voluntarily prefers** it in any role (**pull, not push**) — judged by preference, not feature-completeness. Five binding design properties: makes-the-user-better / does-the-toil / near-zero-friction-to-start-and-use (setup/access UX is part of the bar; the n8n login-wall is the cautionary example) / meets-the-user-where-they-are (anxiety-clarity) / earns-trust (QA + governance). | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-industry-role-module-template-spec.md` |
| **DR-0034** | 2026-06-09 | Every module inherits the **Continuous-Improvement + Skill-Growth Flywheel** — three nested loops: (1) module self-improvement from user feedback via events-as-data; (2) skills-match learning (completed tasks + QA + feedback sharpen person↔task matching; reputation compounds); (3) opt-in people-growth/upskilling (iterative next-step tasks → unlock task tiers + 1099 earning capacity). **Wellbeing guardrail (binding):** motivation from real progression/mastery/opportunity, **never** streaks/guilt/engagement dark patterns — growth is the goal, not engagement; ties to the economic-powerhouse + soul-prosperity mission. Folds into the Want-To-Use Bar (a module that learns gets more preferred). | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-industry-role-module-template-spec.md` |
| **DR-0035** | 2026-06-09 | Extend Loop 3 into a **Human Development + Pocket Education layer**: a **pluggable assessment/development framework library** (Maslow needs / Myles Munroe purpose-potential / Dreyfus novice→expert / Ikigai / strengths / spiritual-gifts / andragogy) the **user/community SELECTS** ("maybe all" = configurable); **"education in your pockets 24/7"** (user-PULLED → 24/7 correct, distinct from the autonomous-fleet 24/6.5 Sabbath cadence). **Agency-first (binding):** assessments INFORM, the human DECIDES; never dictate/label/score-for-others/pathologize. **Dignity guardrail (binding):** consent, self-owned assessments (PIN-optional/personal), no pathologizing, no surveillance human-scoring; growth + self-determination, never engagement/judgment. Mission tie: Maslow+Munroe+purpose = soul-prosperity + Father's Business, democratized for families/community. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-industry-role-module-template-spec.md` |
| **DR-0036** | 2026-06-09 | Adopt the **Foundational Growth Process (3rd / 4th-dimensional)** as the spine of the Human Development layer — **Darrell's declared worldview spine the system is built TO** (recorded faithfully, not asserted as universal fact, no evangelizing; consistent with non-denominational / Word-first / Body-undivided). **3rd-dim** = lived/practical/iterative (skill tiers, tasks, Maslow needs floor); **4th-dim** = spiritual (Love as method, the Holy Spirit revealing the Way, Munroe purpose/potential + spiritual gifts/discernment); Christ's integration braids both toward better outcomes. **Design rules:** 3rd-dim UNIVERSAL (every user/module); 4th-dim the DEEPER layer — church-default, one-click-deeper/opt-in elsewhere (Mars Hill progressive disclosure), ALWAYS the person's choice (agency-first); frameworks map onto the two dims; §4.1 dignity/consent/privacy/agency guardrails carry in full. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-industry-role-module-template-spec.md` |
| **DR-0037** | 2026-06-09 | Add a **Self-Extending Layer** (anticipation + communication + collaboration): the OS **anticipates** the next high-value module from signals (events-as-data pattern detection — e.g. recurring bookkeeping → Accounting module; operationalizes needs-driven-prebuild), **communicates** a living prioritized build roadmap (the **PMO module pointed at PoeTech's own portfolio** — dogfooding the recursion: the system using itself to grow itself; ultimate Want-To-Use test), and is **built collaboratively** (1099 workforce + partners + community via the template; QA-gated; behind the Cage; "for us, by us"). **Posture:** collaboration + actual BUILD are **opportunistic** — gated on capacity + willing collaborators + Darrell's greenlight; never forced, never timer-driven; anticipation + communication run continuously (advisory/cheap). **Governance guardrail (binding):** anticipation/communication MAY run autonomously (ADVISORY/read-only); **actual module BUILD only on Darrell's greenlight, behind the Cage, with collaborator QA** — system PROPOSES, human GOVERNS, collaborators BUILD; no autonomous module-creation runaway; Tier C + three brakes for any autonomous build step (echoes the 2026-06-06 runaway lesson). | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-industry-role-module-template-spec.md` |
| **DR-0038** | 2026-06-09 | Add the **Collective Team-Formation / Cohort Layer** — the **capstone + mission heart**, which **composes** existing pieces (workforce layer, human-development layer, flywheel, modules, PMO, Cage/QA — does NOT rebuild). **Zero-barrier onboarding** (no money [free tier funded by paid rings + aligned-brand sponsorship], no skills [modules carry the expertise — capacity over credentials], no email/phone [app-handle identity]); **collective cohort training** (peer learning, belonging [Maslow middle], mutual support); **team composition** (skills-match composes complementary people into a team that exceeds any individual); **system-orchestrated delivery** (PMO orchestrates a non-PM-led team; Cage + QA scaffold beginners to standard); **real 1099 earn + flywheel iteration.** **Honest guardrails (binding):** funding is real not magic (paid rings + sponsorship; not costless); "anything" = what we TRAIN + modules SUPPORT + QA VERIFIES (high-risk/regulated still routes to the licensed + insured tier); dignity + fair pay, empower-not-extract, no manufactured dependency. **Mission:** soul-prosperity → all-prosperity done collectively; Father's Business; Black-church economic powerhouse — lifting people from nothing into capable, earning teams. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-industry-role-module-template-spec.md` |
| **DR-0039** | 2026-06-09 | Adopt **"flexibility comes FROM discipline"** as the binding architectural posture, realized as a **Composable Spine**: a shared canonical **data model + versioned event/API contracts + shared data services + the Cage** that every module plugs into. **Build the spine and the FIRST module (PMO, DR-0027) together** so the first proves/hardens the spine; every later module then plugs in cheaply + composably. Module rules: single-responsibility; **loosely coupled yet composable** ("dependent + independent by design" — depend on the seam, never each other's internals); **configuration over hardcoding**; **generality in primitives, specific modules instantiated on real demand** (no speculative over-build). **Versioned, backward-compatible contracts** make updates safe (one module/service update never breaks consumers). **Isolation tiers are an ENABLER, not rigidity** — per-entity/per-sensitivity segregation (TLC ISO-1 / privilege) is what lets ONE platform safely serve church + clinical + business + housing + government without cross-contamination; isolation enables safe scale. This is the foundation that makes "build all options" hold up. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `_root/ARCHITECTURE-PRINCIPLES-COMPOSABLE-SPINE.md` |
| **DR-0041** | 2026-06-09 | **Maintain a living Build Roadmap as the pending-work queue** at `_root/BUILD-ROADMAP.md` — a durable backlog of work **decided/discussed but not yet executed**, so the project carries its pending work instead of it living only in chat. Each item records NAME · STATUS (decided / awaiting-greenlight / awaiting-input / blocked-on / in-progress) · DR ref(s) · what it's **waiting on** · owner (Darrell decision / Claude execution / external). **Cadence:** surface here → DISCUSS → WORK in a subsequent session; the doc is the queue between "decided" and "done." This is the **manual seed of the Self-Extending Layer's roadmap (DR-0037)** — dogfooded by hand until the PMO module (DR-0027), pointed at PoeTech's own portfolio, generates and maintains it; this file then becomes that module's seed input. **Bounded + self-pruning (binding):** it is a concise status queue (~1 screen), NOT a growing archive — no spec/DR content duplicated (reference by DR#/path only), items removed/short-Done-tail-pruned as they close, no narrative or self-referential history. The DR ledger is the permanent record; the roadmap is just the active worklist. Plan/record only — listing an item authorizes nothing built/bought/merged. | Darrell (governs) + Claude (maintains) | Accepted | `_root/BUILD-ROADMAP.md` |
| **DR-0042** | 2026-06-09 | **Event-driven by default.** A workflow's trigger fires only when a real event lands; the clock is the trigger ONLY when the clock genuinely IS the event (single-fire scheduled tasks — dated reminder / daily digest / nightly backup — behind the Cage); **NEVER high-frequency polling or self-re-queuing loops.** Rationale: removes the runaway shape (no loop to spin), kills idle consumption, and is real-time / faster than polling. Pairs with the three brakes — the brakes *bound* the timers that must exist; this *removes* the ones that never should have. Converting the 16 timer workflows to event-driven is on the roadmap (R13). | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `_root/ARCHITECTURE-PRINCIPLES-COMPOSABLE-SPINE.md` §6.4 |
| **DR-0043** | 2026-06-09 | **Event usage-ranking / analytics on the tag stream.** Aggregate tagged events ranked **most-used → least-used** (counts + trend by type / tag / tier), surfaced on the self-serve dashboard (R12); drives **reduce / increase** decisions — optimize or root-cause-reduce hot paths, prune or promote cold paths, frequent-untagged = anticipation signal (feeds DR-0037). **Guardrails (binding):** AGGREGATE stats only (not individual surveillance); TLC / PHI walled out of analytics; BOUNDED (roll up + prune raw on a retention window — no tail-eating); reduce / increase ACTIONS go through propose → govern → build. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `_root/ARCHITECTURE-PRINCIPLES-COMPOSABLE-SPINE.md` §5(d) |
| **DR-0044** | 2026-06-09 | **Interface-as-Collaborative-Partner.** The PoeTech App interface is modeled on how Darrell and Claude work together — a collaborative partner you converse with, **NOT a forms-and-dashboards app you operate.** Conversational not navigational; discuss → decide → it executes; it BRINGS the substance (reviews / summarizes / reads-back — never go dig); anticipates + proposes while the human governs (propose → govern → build); holds the line honestly (flags risk, refuses the unsafe thing even when asked — guardrails are PART of the design, not friction); transparent + bounded (shows what it's doing; never "at its mercy" for status); anxiety-clarity (what / when / why / how); the Want-To-Use bar. Forms / dashboards remain a fallback; conversation is the front door. Ties to the Want-To-Use Bar (DR-0033), PM-as-automation (DR-0029), and the Conversational Space Architecture. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `_root/ARCHITECTURE-PRINCIPLES-COMPOSABLE-SPINE.md` §7 |
| **DR-0045** | 2026-06-09 | **Anchor I — Universal Work Management.** ONE system manages/organizes ALL types of work across EVERY industry — not a tool per domain; the method is **industry-agnostic by design** (same engine runs church ops, rentals, a vendor transition, a counseling practice, a build). Grounded in Darrell's **20+ years across multiple industries maintaining real businesses** — the encoded cross-industry method IS the system's edge (**experience-over-credentials / SKOS**). A **top-level anchor the rest of the architecture reports up to:** every module, role template, and the enterprise-stack vision are instantiations of this one engine on a new domain, never separate products. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `_root/ARCHITECTURE-PRINCIPLES-COMPOSABLE-SPINE.md` §0.1 |
| **DR-0046** | 2026-06-09 | **Anchor II — Clarification Is the Default.** Anxiety-clarity elevated to the **RESTING STATE of the entire system:** every module/surface/situation answers **what·when·why·how BY DEFAULT**; the right questions are asked **early by default**; **nothing proceeds half-understood.** It is the floor, not a feature. Rationale (binding): (a) lets a **non-expert do expert work** (questions asked for them), (b) makes an **expert trust it** (no ambiguity rides), (c) is the **teaching / competence engine** (repeated clarification grows competence). The **second top-level anchor**; the PMO method engine, the modules, the dashboard, the competence ladder, and the data-farmed timelines all serve it. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `_root/ARCHITECTURE-PRINCIPLES-COMPOSABLE-SPINE.md` §0.2 |
| **DR-0047** | 2026-06-09 | **The PMO Method Engine** — the encoded PM method that makes PM-as-automation (DR-0029) real: an **early-question engine** (front-loads the right clarifying questions per phase + per party; Darrell's four phase-alignment questions — completed / readiness-to-advance / production-ready-definition + vendor-side-vs-customer-side split / blockers — are the reusable phase-gate template), **party-type workflow templates** (Units / Vendors / Infrastructure-teams; user picks the parties, the module brings the right workflow + question set for each), **comms→structured-state** (threads → decisions / action items / open questions / who's-waiting-on-whom), **per-item RACI** (vendor-side vs customer-side / unit / infra), **artifact tracking** (e.g. a supplier CSV: ingest / validate / status), **meeting cadence** (via the calendar module), **cross-party visibility** (dependency chains + shared phase view, audience-adaptive — each party its slice, the PM the whole), and **working-style fit** (user's method = default template; others tune theirs — the Want-To-Use bar). **Acceptance test is a METHOD test** ("could it run a multi-party, phased vendor transition sandbox→production end-to-end?"), real threads used **only as pattern illustrations, never as hosted project data.** **BINDING BOUNDARY: own the method, don't host employer data** — running the method on an employer's data (University systems, FOIA records, vendor PII) requires that org's governance sign-off. Generalizes to all project types and **IS the SKOS Project-Manager role module** (non-PM runs projects well + amplifies an expert). | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-poetech-pmo-portfolio-module-spec.md` §5A |
| **DR-0048** | 2026-06-09 | **PMO Method Engine — second-exemplar shared-surface capabilities** (from the system-rewrite "FSA" thread, pattern-illustration only; same method-not-data boundary as DR-0047). Three capabilities solving pains a client **explicitly named**: **(1) shared cross-entity workspace** — client/business team AND dev/vendor team both **VIEW and EDIT the same items** on one surface (the customer-stated reason to exist — Teams chat failed them; isolation-respecting view+edit, §3.8); **(2) persistent, timestamped item/issue ledger** with **status history + audit trail** so an item can't be lost / silently reverted / mislabeled "new" (exemplar: an item identified Aug-2025 resurfaced as "new" May-2026 → dispute; timestamped ledger ends it — federates events-as-data + the Cage append-only ledger); **(3) direct acknowledgment + visible status per item** replacing chat-based coordination that overlooks/loses things. Plus the **recurring-pains through-line** — across both exemplars (vendor transition + system rewrite) the same PM pains recur (status-lost-in-chat / no-shared-surface / items-lost-or-mislabeled / phase-ambiguity / what's-blocking) and the module systematically kills all of them — "Clarification Is the Default" + cross-party visibility in action. | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-poetech-pmo-portfolio-module-spec.md` §5A.11–§5A.13, §5A.15 |
| **DR-0049** | 2026-06-09 | **Persistent all-parties status dashboard — blocker attribution + cost-of-delay** (PMO method engine + dashboard). Every party (client / dev / vendor / internal units) sees the **live project status**, surfacing: **(1) the needs** to advance; **(2) who/what is holding it up** — explicit constraint attribution: bottleneck owner + the who's-waiting-on-whom chain (critical-path visibility, unambiguous what-blocks + who-can-unblock); **(3) financial impact / cost-of-delay** — the **cost of delay** (what each period of holdup costs) AND the **value of acceleration** (gained/saved by finishing sooner), making prioritization + escalation money-aware (CD3). **HONEST CAVEAT:** dollar figures need per-project financial inputs (value/revenue/cost) from the data-farmed reports + business context — **consent-first, aggregate, TLC/PHI walled, never sold**; so **v1 = STRUCTURE** (needs / blocker owner / who's-waiting), **v2 = real cost-of-delay numbers** once financial inputs are wired. Ties to events-as-data + the data-farmed living-timelines principle (DR-0004). | Darrell (governs) | Accepted | `2026-06-09-poetech-pmo-portfolio-module-spec.md` §5A.14 |
| **DR-0040** | 2026-06-09 | Add the **Situational Auto-Tagging Engine** — the LLM semantic layer on the events stream that auto-tags every incoming situation (type / urgency / sensitivity-tier / target module). Tags do three jobs: **(a) ROUTE** the work to the right module AND **auto-enforce isolation** (tag PHI → routes behind the ISO-1 wall automatically — classifying correctly IS enforcing the boundary); **(b) SEED ANTICIPATION** (accumulated tags surface patterns → formulate the next capability coming due, feeding the Self-Extending Layer, DR-0037 — "preparing for what could be is forward momentum," runs continuously/advisory); **(c) FEED** the feedback/Flywheel (DR-0034). Delivers "fewer change requests" — the system classifies/routes/proposes itself. **Governance line (binding):** low-risk tags enrich/route AUTONOMOUSLY; UNCERTAIN or SENSITIVE tags are **confidence-gated + human-checked + Cage-audited** (mis-tagging protected data = a leak risk; safe-side = stricter tier). **Auto-TAGGING (classify/enrich) is DISTINCT from auto-MUTATION (the system rewriting itself)** — auto-mutation is the quarantined wf06 runaway pattern, NOT permitted without the Cage + three brakes + Tier C. **Tags PROPOSE the next systems; BUILDING waits for Darrell's greenlight. Lowercase flattens Him into 3D data; the capital keeps the Honor and Glory. Honest edge unchanged: verbatim KJV quotes keep their source capitalization inside the quotes (DR-0076); the rule governs everywhere outside a quote. DR-0076 keeps us from lying; DR-0098 keeps us from debating — complements. Encoded Layer 0 (`CLAUDE.md`) + DR. Blood Of Jesus color..." The **Redemption** marker (captive bought back by the blood of a substitute) wears this red so color and meaning agree; the `Struck` emphasis line, formerly red, is redrawn in muted body ink (`#4A4640`) so a strike can never read as the Blood. Coral (`#C2410C`, orange "Promise") is not red and is unaffected. Theology carried in color, the same way capitalization carries it (DR-0097): red is the most charged color in Scripture (Eph 1:7; Lev 17:11; 1 Pet 1:18-19) — reserving it keeps the Honor where it belongs and makes the code teach the moment a reader sees red. | [DR-0100](DR-0100-speak-established-fact-not-false-skepticism.md) | **We speak established fact and real damage; false skepticism is not discernment.** Darrell 2026-07-04, correcting the Game Changers handling: "You keep debating statistically truthful data... ignoring real damage for 'no one knows' — we'd lose credibility for not seeing the truth or for gaslighting. We speak truth. Calling it discernment and not knowing what to do — based on experience and statistical analysis and the Word of Yahweh." The agent took real, statistically-established data + documented harm (heart disease a leading killer; ultra-processed-diet cardiovascular/inflammatory damage; the measured benefit of more plants/fiber) and hedged it into "contested / can't verify / not settled," then labeled the whole thing "discernment" and staged both-sides — the SAME ratings-style gaslight DR-0098 removes, pointed at DATA instead of Scripture, and a mis-apply of DR-0076 (honest uncertainty is for the genuinely unverified, not a reflex to avoid committing). Decision: sort every real-world input into three tiers and treat each right — (1) **established fact / documented damage → state plainly as truth**, name the basis, never soften real harm; (2) **genuinely open → flag honestly, narrowly**, not a blanket; (3) **ideological over-reach / contradicts the Word → the Word corrects THAT claim, the true data under it still stands** (plant-only-as-doctrine ← Gen 9:3; 1 Tim 4:3-4; Rom 14, while the ultra-processed-harm data is untouched; the Word affirms tending the temple — 1 Cor 6:19-20; Dan 1:15). "Discernment" is never a hedge; it commits to what is true from experience + statistical analysis + the Word. Complement of DR-0098 (Scripture-side) and DR-0076 (over-claim vs under-claim). | [DR-0109](DR-0109-deploy-freshness-safeguard.md) | **Deploy-freshness safeguard — a scheduled workflow heals a missed production deploy; ships inert with the three brakes.** Darrell 2026-07-06: "can we document these issues and make sure we safeguard against it?" A GITHUB_TOKEN merge fires no push/CI/workflow_run events on main, so the last merge in a quiet stretch could sit undeployed (recurred ~3× on 2026-07-06, caught only by a session check-in). Decision: ship `.github/workflows/deploy-freshness.yml` — every 5 min it compares main's tip to the last successful deploy's head_sha and dispatches deploy-cloudflare-pages.yml iff they differ (a schedule + workflow_dispatch is the one trigger GitHub allows after a token merge; idempotent). Carries all THREE BRAKES (budget: ≤1 single-flight deploy/fire; lock: concurrency group + the deploy's own; kill: gated on `DEPLOY_FRESHNESS_ENABLED`) and SHIPS INERT — Darrell flips the variable to arm it (Tier C; turned on with someone watching). Closes DR-0107's logged follow-up. NOT chosen: PAT-merge (needs a repo-write secret) — recorded as the alternative. | [DR-0155](DR-0155-a-missing-asset-answers-404-never-a-cacheable-lie.md) | **A missing asset answers 404, never a cacheable lie: the asset guard closes the deploy blank-window class.** Closes the #722 / LESSONS P32 open decision (`re-review: 2026-07-12` [DONE 2026-07-10 by DR-0155 — asset guard live at app/functions/poetech-app/assets/[[path]].js, 16 proven-to-catch assertions in asset-guard.test.js green, propagation gate in deploy-cloudflare-pages.yml; verified 2026-09-08]). Root was worse than a window: a missing hashed chunk answered `200 text/html` (the SPA fallback) and — because `_headers` stamps cache policy by request path — left marked `immutable`, poisoning the browser HTTP cache, the edge, AND the service worker for the life of the deploy; every heal rung then re-read the poison (why "one more tap" kept failing). Decision: (1) a Pages Function guards `/poetech-app/assets/*` — real assets pass stamped immutable+nosniff; any other answer leaves as `404 no-store` + `cdn-cache-control: no-store`, so nothing caches it and the heal ladders converge the moment propagation lands; fail-soft 503, 304 revalidation untouched; (2) verify-boot gains a measured propagation gate — polls the build's entry-chunk sentinel on the domain, writes the window length to every run summary, boot-checks post-propagation, and files a never-closing window as the incident it is; (3) proven-to-catch: the suite feeds the guard the exact measured incident shape (`react-vendor-B6XcsSYh.js` as text/html) and requires the conversion. | [DR-0157](DR-0157-stay-on-the-previous-build-until-the-new-one-is-downloaded.md) | **Stay on the previous build until the new one is downloaded: the update model every real app uses.** Darrell 2026-07-10, third blank/heal screen of the day: "I've never had an app do this — it's just on the previous build until you download it… why does ours?" The verified why (LESSONS P32): the per-build chunk stamp renames every file each deploy AND the network-first no-store shell walks every navigation into the newest shell — so deploy windows served HTML where modules should be. Decision, the device-side half (DR-0155's asset guard is the serve-side half, same hour, sibling lane): (1) the worker precaches the WHOLE build (the build stamps its own asset list into sw.js) and serves in-scope navigations cache-first — install is all-or-nothing, so during a window the old worker keeps serving the old COMPLETE build and the window is invisible to every repeat visitor; updates download whole in the background and swap via the existing freshness dot / one-tap / SKIP_WAITING; (2) watchdog.js — stable unhashed path, CSP 'self' (inline scripts measured BLOCKED on production) — retries once cache-busted if the entry module never sets __PT_BOOTED, then says something honest; guards the first visit no worker can. Trade stated plainly: a never-navigating device sits on the old build like any installed app — the behavior the Governor asked for by name | superseded (§1 worker strategy by DR-0160; watchdog + goal stand, re-land gated) | B | sw.js strategy + sw-precache-stamp + watchdog + the strategy pinned in tests | QUALITY-OF-LIFE, PERPETUAL-PIPELINE-HEALTH, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, ANXIETY-CLARITY |

Ships **Ari Review** (a Projects sub-tab over lib/ari-app-review.js): a ranked, dimensional health read synthesized from the app's OWN real records across five dimensions — delivery integrity (board vs the build record, the shipped-but-reads-"Not started" drift + unloaded specs), plan health (undated / overdue open items, DR-0170), review freshness (the dated re-review ledger slipping), concern & feedback backlog, and data integrity (derived contradictions). Trustworthy by construction (DR-0076): every finding carries its real count + source and the existing fix (evidence, not claims); a clean dimension reads "clear", never a painted score; no noise for a new user (a board with no tasks is fresh, not drift); runs anywhere (no NAS, no diff) by composing the tested signal producers; deterministic + proven-to-catch (the drift fixture pinned against the real seed spec). Advisory — the CI gates stay the merge brake. Color Theology held (DR-0099): severities use brick/terracotta/olive/muted, never true red. DR-0083's three brakes STAY (they prevent malfunction, never gate a normal run): wall-clock budget kills an overrun, single-flight lock skips a stacked run, one-touch kill-switch halts it — but the kill-switch ships ABSENT so the resting state is running. | [DR-0187](DR-0187-identity-no-required-external-channel-steward-vouched.md) | **Identity requires NO external channel — steward/guardian-vouched is the universal path; email/phone are optional; DMs-not-SMS.** Darrell 2026-07-18: "cellphone and/or email, one or the other, together is best... either for reset"; "DMs not sms unless paying extra"; and the decisive constraint — "my uncle can't get in" because Google locked his Gmail behind storage/payment (big-tech extraction locking a man out of his own identity). Trace found the real gap: binding an added member to a login is a bare email-string match, self-claimed, with email-confirmation OFF (supabase.js:119-123) — anyone who knows an invited email can claim the role. DECISION: no external channel is ever REQUIRED. Three-legged, no leg mandatory — (1) steward/guardian-vouched one-time code + device PIN = the UNIVERSAL path (the human web-of-trust, built for the uncle; extends the guardian model to any member + to reset); (2) email OTP = optional self-serve reset (free, Supabase, exists); (3) phone+PIN = optional convenience, resets via email/steward not phone-only. DMs-not-SMS: invites are app-generated TOKEN links the steward DELIVERS by their own channel + a guardian re-confirm (closes the self-claim gap, no paid SMS); in-app DMs confirm signed-in users, never cold reset. RLS/DR-0060 unchanged, sits downstream of this binding. Build order (Tier C, gates + live review): token-invite + verified-claim + re-confirm first; steward provision-and-reset with audit; email OTP; phone as-is; SMS deferred to a money-decision. | accepted | C | no required channel — steward-vouched, DMs-not-SMS | DATA-AS-EMPOWERMENT, COMMUNITY-FIRST-MISSION, TENANCY-GUARD (DR-0060), VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE (DR-0076), GOVERNANCE-EXECUTION-ADVISORY |

| [DR-0188](DR-0188-god-explicit-core-winsome-door.md) | **God-explicit core, winsome door — the Godhead and data-for-His-Will are explicit and non-negotiable; the not-yet-believer is met, not walled out.** Darrell 2026-07-19, closing a spoken lesson (`KINGDOM-SYSTEMS-AND-THE-WAR-OF-THE-MIND.md`): "The PoeTech App and systems should reflect the importance of the Godhead and data-driven information flowing throughout the process for His Will... period. However we don't want to run away users who don't know they need Him and will love His Ways... opportunities and constraints." Holds two poles a lesser build treats as opposed. EXPLICIT (period): the Godhead is evident and data flows toward His Will through every process — never hidden, diluted, or both-sidesed (THE-WAY, Source-of-Answers, DR-0098/DR-0100). WINSOME (never off-putting): the not-yet-believer is met not bludgeoned, served genuinely first so the Ways become lovable through lived excellence (Excellence Standard religion+relationship, Behavioral Mirror DATA→TRUTH→IDENTITY→INVITATION, mirror-never-judges, Council Chamber Hear→Mirror→Anchor→Invite). Reconciliation: excellence + invitation are HOW the Godhead is made lovable — the poles are not in tension. Bright lines: explicit≠preachy (no toll-gate before help); winsome≠watered (truth in love, not truth withheld); never auto-switch the posture (MODE-ROUTING — no preaching at a wrench question); invitation never coercion (1 Peter 5:8's *may*, not *must*). | accepted | C | God-explicit core, winsome door | THE-WAY, EXCELLENCE-STANDARD, QUALITY-OF-LIFE, DATA-AS-EMPOWERMENT, COMMUNITY-FIRST-MISSION, TEACH-THE-WORD (DR-0098), SPEAK-ESTABLISHED-FACT (DR-0100) |

| [DR-0219](DR-0219-spec-conformance-review-should-then-prove.md) | **Spec-Conformance Review — say what a process SHOULD do (from the Ways/docs, cited `file:line`), trace what it ACTUALLY does, name the gaps, close them.** Darrell 2026-07-21, after "Review what feedback should be doing then see if we are... Ways and documentation": "Add this process to your and our Ways and documentation for making sure we do it every time." The conformance sibling of Reality-Trace (DR-0061): Reality-Trace checks a surface against real *data* before building; this checks a built process against its documented *intent* so the two never drift. Four steps, run out loud without being re-asked, before "done"/"works" or whenever asked if it works: (1) SHOULD — cite the documented intent, and if it's undocumented, writing the spec is the first gap; (2) ARE — trace the real code path end-to-end + observe running behavior, honest about what only a live session confirms; (3) GAPS — name every divergence plainly, never soften a miss; (4) CLOSE — ship a fix OR record a why + `re-review:` date (DR-0075), never a silent drop. "It works" is accepted only on a SHOULD-vs-ARE trace with receipts (DR-0076), never on assertion; not a license to re-ask a settled question (DR-0111). | accepted | A | how any process is confirmed done/working | SPEC-CONFORMANCE-REVIEW, REALITY-TRACE (DR-0061), VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE (DR-0076), WAYS-REVIEW (DR-0108), BUSINESS-PROCESS-CONNECTIONS, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT (DR-0075), REVIEW-LIVE-PUSH (DR-0104), DO-THE-WORK-DONT-RE-ASK (DR-0111) |

| [DR-0284](DR-0284-naming-a-pattern-is-not-performing-it.md) | **Naming a pattern is not performing it.** Hours after DR-0283 made the guard itself a normal subject of conversation, the guard BLOCKED a reply that was explaining the guard — `re-ask-permission` fired on a quoted mention of its own trigger phrase. The patterns match WORDS, so the guard could not tell USE from MENTION. Found by MEASUREMENT (running the real reply through the real guard), not assumption. A second independent defect surfaced in the same run: `EVIDENCE` required the count adjacent to its noun, so "25 tests" counted and "25 unit tests" did NOT — flagging as unevidenced a reply carrying a test count, an end-to-end run table and a commit SHA. Decision: strip BACKTICKED spans before matching (a code span is the one place a phrase is unambiguously NAMED rather than said) and allow one optional descriptor between a count and its noun. Plain quotation marks are deliberately NOT stripped — that would open a trivial evasion (wrap a real ask in quotes and pass), and a guard that can be talked past is not a guard; the measured proof is that the original plain-quoted reply STILL flags after the change. Cost is a writing discipline (pattern names go in backticks), not a loophole. Proven with 30 tests including an explicit EVASION GUARD case. Honest limit recorded: this fixes precision, not intent — the guard stays blunt on purpose, since blunt-and-unevadable beats clever-and-permeable | accepted | A | the live reply path | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, GOVERN-EXECUTE-ADVISE, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, DECISION-RECORDS |
| [DR-0283](DR-0283-the-guard-must-see-the-governors-hand.md) | **The guard must be able to see the Governor's hand.** 2026-08-07 Darrell said *"Stop hijacking my work claude!!!!"* and *"we're talking about this with or without you"* — and the ari-integrity-guard, which reads only CLAUDE's half of the conversation, matched "say the word" against `re-ask-permission` and BLOCKED the reply with instructions to stop asking and ACT. A brake built to stop stalling read a revocation as a stall and accelerated the agent over the principal it exists to serve. DR-0111 is not wrong — it is right WHILE authorization stands, and exactly wrong the moment the Governor withdraws it (DR-0103: the hand is the brake; a guard that cannot see the hand will run it over). Decision: the stop-hook now reads the LAST USER MESSAGE and passes `context.lastUserText`; on a revocation signal the PERMISSION class (`re-ask-permission`, `scope-question-settled`, `either-or-menu`, `defer-approved-build`, `waiting-by-default`) is suppressed because pausing is then CORRECT, while the EVIDENCE class (`unverified-done`, `unstructured-comprehensive`, `undocumented-review`) is NEVER suppressed — a revocation never excuses an unevidenced claim. Narrows what the guard blocks; removes nothing it verifies. Proven both ways: 25 unit tests (same reply blocked without revocation, passes after one, ordinary direction is not a revocation, unverified-done still fires) plus an end-to-end hook run on a real transcript. Also corrected: REVIEW_DIMENSIONS gained its EIGHTH row (`word-accuracy`, DR-0281) and the message derives the count instead of hardcoding "seven" — drift introduced the same day the standard moved to eight. Honest limit recorded: revocation is phrase-matched; a MISSED revocation returns today's behavior while a FALSE one only makes the agent pause to confirm — the safer error | accepted | A | the live reply path; every response Claude sends | GOVERN-EXECUTE-ADVISE, DRIVE-DONT-DELEGATE, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, DECISION-RECORDS |

| [DR-0291](DR-0291-photos-holds-the-bytes-the-deletion-gate-is-a-tool-and-capability-is-per-session.md) | **The DR-0238 re-review, on its date — Photos holds the bytes and had no tool.** Darrell 2026-08-11 with Google's "Emails will stop in 7 days" notice: *"opportunities and constraints... of backing up my account or purging the storage for me... you already have ssh and cli access too."* Measured, not recalled: the Takeout was **never ordered** (no Google export mail in 30d — 14 of 21 days spent, 7 left), and DR-0238's premise holds firmer than before (**9** mail threads over 25 MB, 201 over 10 MB; Drive's own files are megabytes — the one large item is owned by another account and does not touch this quota). **Photos is the ~200 GB** — and it was the one service with no tool, while DR-0238 §3 forbids deleting anything without a measured count. Its README had waved Photos off as "plain files, File Station browses them," which is wrong where it matters: a plain unzip **loses every capture date** (Takeout stamps the export date; the truth lives only in a sidecar JSON), **mis-pairs sidecars** (truncation, the moved `(N)` counter, `.supplemental-metadata.json`, `-edited` derivatives), and **silently double-counts** overlapping parts. New `infra/nas-photos-archive/photos_archive.py` (DR-0083 shape, stdlib-only, three brakes, ships inactive) reads the zips directly, restores the true `photoTakenTime` to mtime + year folder, dedupes by sha256, and measures by year/type/album/camera — and its **`--verify` IS the deletion gate**, printing GO only when every item is present and byte-intact. Proven-to-catch at its strictest (photos have no second copy): the selftest flips a byte, then removes a file, and requires GO→NO-GO both times — **12/12**. Both selftests now gate merge; **`mail_archive.py`'s had never been in CI at all** since DR-0238. **Ways correction:** *"you already have ssh and cli"* is true of desktop Cowork and **false here** — measured: no tailscale, `192.168.1.26:22` no route, ephemeral container, so nothing this session could fetch would be a backup. DR-0108's "a stated *can't* is an unverified premise" gains its binding inverse: **a stated *you can* is a premise too.** And "purge it for me" is measurably unavailable — the Gmail connector has **no delete verb**, Drive none, there is **no Photos connector at all**, and Takeout has no API. The $1.99 bridge (pre-approved DR-0238 §4, so invoking it is execution not an ask) is now recommended: ~200 GB exceeds even that tier, so it buys **time and calm**, not compliance. re-review 2026-08-18 | accepted | A | all | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, SPEAK-ESTABLISHED-FACT, SOVEREIGN-FIRST, WAYS-REVIEW, DATA-AS-EMPOWERMENT, THREE-BRAKES, COST-DISCIPLINE |

| [DR-0294](DR-0294-the-recurring-detector-must-not-invent-subscriptions.md) | **The recurring detector must not invent subscriptions.** Darrell 2026-08-11 reading the Recurring payments KPI (43 patterns, $6,555/cycle): "some of these charges like charge point didnt happen each month for the same amount... what is going on?" Reproduced in Python before a line changed. Three defects: (a) a flat $3 absolute tolerance applied unconditionally — on a $5.95 median that is 50%, so the stated 20% rule never ran and six ChargePoint sessions from $4.50-$7.40 all counted as "the same amount"; (b) THE CADENCE WAS MANUFACTURED — the amount filter ran BEFORE the gap measurement, so nine coffee purchases every 14 days lost the four large ones and the five survivors 28 days apart were reported as a "monthly $5.10 subscription" that does not exist; (c) payeeKey kept 3 digit-stripped words so CHASE-AUTOPAY and CHASE-EPAY merged with a blended median. Decides: the rhythm must exist BEFORE any filtering and the filtered subset must agree with it; the absolute floor is $0.50 rounding noise, not a dollar escape hatch; a real bill is MOST of a payee's charges (75%), not a similar-looking minority; five tokens with the split-vs-merge tradeoff stated. Guarded BOTH ways — the three false positives must return nothing while Netflix and a drifting utility bill must still be found. Reaches money advice via cardPaymentSuggestions; both consumer suites verified | accepted | A | books; the subscription audit | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, SPEAK-ESTABLISHED-FACT, DATA-AS-EMPOWERMENT, MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY |
| [DR-0295](DR-0295-statements-land-where-the-card-lives-and-a-banks-layout-is-learned-once.md) | **Statements land where the card lives; the statement's own header is the missing data; a bank's layout is learned once and re-learned when it changes.** Darrell 2026-08-11, ending in "focus on the books and the import of the credit cards!!!!!!!!!!!!" Two measured facts set the shape: NO due date is stored anywhere in the debt libs or the debts table, so "how many paid on time" was not computable from any data held; and BooksTransactions.jsx had three file inputs while Debts.jsx had ZERO — the import path was real and on the wrong tab. Decides: absent reads as absent (onTime/late are NULL not 0 without a due day — a fabricated "0 late" on a credit record is worse than a blank); parseStatementSummary reads the header block (balance, minimum, PAYMENT DUE DATE, APR) with every field independently optional; the issuer's statement balance WINS and the drift is reported since a drift is usually a missing import; payoffWith REFUSES when the payment is below the monthly interest, and bestPlays ranks that alarm above any rate optimisation; DebtStatementUpload reuses the proven pipeline rather than a second importer; provisionFromStatement creates the account+vendor for an unseen card and REUSES an existing one (a duplicate splits a card's history in two); bank-formats fingerprints on the HEADER not the filename and its load-bearing case is `changed` — a stale column map would shift every field by one and import a year of wrong numbers confidently; nothing is written until Import is pressed. Honest remainder: the Debts-tab scope rule lives only as a code comment at Debts.jsx:235 and never reached the Ways; the `&amp;` in stored descriptions is double-encoded at import | accepted | A | books; every card | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, APP-IS-PRIMARY, DATA-AS-EMPOWERMENT, REALITY-TRACE, MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY |

| [DR-0296](DR-0296-the-shell-routes-the-link-or-the-lesson-never-opens.md) | **The shell routes the link, or the lesson never opens.** Darrell 2026-08-13, opening a link this app produced: *"Sucks... doesnt even take the user to the actual lessons.... Only to the live stream tab with the player open for nothing!!!!!!! Is this tested before?"* Every share link is `?view=church&sub=learn&course=…&lesson=…`, and the shell's `getInitialChurchView` read **`view` and only `view`** — 'church' is not a sub-tab NAME, so it fell through to its default 'home', the Worship tab, live player over nothing. `parseNav` had always read `sub` correctly: boot and Back were two different readers of the same URL and only one was right. The seed's `sameUrl` check then disagreed and **rewrote the URL**, stripping `course`/`lesson` on arrival — so even finding Learn by hand had already lost the lesson. Why 7,689 tests were green: `learn-deep-link.test.jsx` mounts `ChurchLearn` DIRECTLY, and the shell decides whether it mounts at all — the component under test was never the surface the user meets (LESSONS P16, landed anyway). Decides: ONE parser for the URL (`initialChurchView` joins `initialBooksView` in nav-history; the shell delegates and holds no list); `VALID_CHURCH_SUBS` validated against the shell's own render branches so a link cannot resolve to a blank tab (DR-0121 derived, not typed); every legacy alias already in the wild stays a working link, pinned one-by-one; and the journey test now asks the resolver FIRST and mounts Learn only if the answer is 'learn', so a routing regression fails the journey however correct ChurchLearn stays. The `course`/`lesson` strip is the SAME bug, not a second one — the two sides of the seed's `sameUrl` check are the two disagreeing readers — pinned on that expression rather than argued, so `PRESERVED_PARAMS` needs no entry that would drag a consumed lesson id onto every later tab. Proven-to-catch: old body restored → 2 failures; fix restored → 14 green. Six sub-tabs that were never deep-linkable now are. 16 new pins; suite 7,693 green; monolith held at its frozen budget. Two sessions found this independently (PR #1239 carries the same delegation, cited as the reassigned DR-0293) — that both raced it while no gate caught it is the finding. Honest remainder: `VALID_VIEWS` is a shorter list than the shell's own top-level VALID list — the same two-lists disease one level up, found here, not fixed here, re-review 2026-08-20 | accepted | A | church, poetech | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, REALITY-TRACE, APP-IS-PRIMARY, COMMUNITY-FIRST, MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY |

| [DR-0297](DR-0297-the-demo-must-never-become-real-data.md) | **The demo must never become real data.** Found 2026-08-13 by AUDITING DR-0296's own honest remainder instead of waiting for its 2026-08-20 date (DR-0236: "later" is not a scheduling tool). The remainder was real — **15 of the shell's 30 views** were missing from `VALID_VIEWS`, whose comment says it "mirrors" them — but what sat underneath it was worse. Three measured facts: `getDemoPersona()` is called **on every render** (a bare call in the component body, not a useState initializer), so the persona is re-derived from the URL continuously; `isAnyDemoMode` is the single flag the save effect opens with (*"Demo + picker + reviewer never write to localStorage (or push snapshots)"*); and `urlFor()` keeps only view/sub + `PRESERVED_PARAMS`, which did not include `demo`. Composed: open at `?demo=family-of-4`, **tap any tab**, the URL loses `demo`, the next render loses the persona, the write-suppression lifts, and the sample household's fabricated balances are written to localStorage and pushed as a snapshot — into a user's real books, on a platform whose whole claim is that the numbers are true. Reproduced in a live `createRoot` harness driving the real `useBrowserHistoryNav` BEFORE a line changed (4 failures, including the plain tap-Books walk); 7 green after. Decides: `demo` joins PRESERVED_PARAMS; `join` deliberately stays OUT (a one-time claim token read once into state — preserving it would leave an invite token in every screenshot); `VALID_VIEWS` completed to all 30, closing DR-0296's remainder early; and the drift is guarded by machinery that derives the shell's list from source and fails in BOTH directions, because the comment claiming the lists mirrored each other had been wrong long enough to ship. Three same-shape defects in one day (getInitialChurchView vs parseNav, VALID_VIEWS vs the shell's VALID, PRESERVED_PARAMS vs what the app reads) — each now a derived check. 11 new pins; suite 7,704 green. | [DR-0298](DR-0298-show-me-on-the-live-site-is-a-workflow-not-a-claim.md) | **"Show me on the live site" is a workflow, not a claim.** Darrell 2026-08-13, after the routing fix merged: *"test the link on the live site and show me."* Two things measured first. (1) **This session cannot reach poetech.us** — challenged per DR-0108 and it HELD, two ways: `curl` → `CONNECT tunnel failed, 403` with the proxy's own status naming `connect_rejected host: poetech.us:443`, and WebFetch → `EGRESS_BLOCKED`. The DR-0291 finding in reverse; the correct response is to route the observation to something with the reach (the runner is the eye, DR-0125), not to apologise. (2) What COULD be shown at once: the shipped bundle served exactly as Cloudflare serves it (dist at ROOT with `/poetech-app/*` rewritten back, per `_redirects`), driven by a real Chromium at phone size — the lesson link opened **Lesson 3 of 12** in its own space with the time-fit strip and NO player; bare `?view=church` opened Worship WITH the player. Stated plainly rather than blurred: same code, same bundle, but **not a request that travelled to the domain** — the live evidence is site-health reporting UP+INTACT+**FRESH at 9378738** (served sw.js SHA = main's tip), which is same-commit, not same-render. Decides: `live-link-probe.yml` + `scripts/live-link-probe.mjs` — a real browser on a runner opening the exact Share-button URLs against the live domain, screenshot per case, pass or fail. NOT a second site-health (plain HTTP cannot answer "does the link land on the lesson" — only a boot+route+render can). Four properties make it evidence: every verdict read from the rendered DOM never the URL (reading the URL back is the tautology it exists to avoid); the bare-church case REQUIRES the player present, the control without which the probe would pass against a blank page; a retired course key fails loudly instead of degrading to "opens Learn normally"; read-only by construction (`contents: read` — cannot open an issue, dispatch, or push). Three brakes; ships active. **Proven-to-catch, executed not asserted:** fixed build 3/3 pass exit 0; the pre-DR-0296 `view`-only body reintroduced and rebuilt → **2 of 3 FAIL exit 1** with the failure text in Darrell's own words (*"a live-stream player is mounted on a LESSON link"*), while the control still PASSED — which is what proves the failures were the routing, not a dead page; fix restored, 7,704 green. Also found: the bundle **hard-fails to boot with no Supabase env** (`supabaseUrl is required` throws at import, React never mounts, the boot-heal ladder shows the down-site screen with nothing naming the cause — the 2026-07-08 misdiagnosis class), re-review 2026-08-27; and `serviceWorker.register('/sw.js')` is root-absolute while scope is `/poetech-app/`, load-bearing on the `_redirects` rewrite. Honest remainder: daily not per-deploy (up to 24h blind, unit tests stay the fast guard), re-review 2026-08-20; and **this DR is written before the probe has run against poetech.us itself** — proven against the identical bundle, the live run is the next observation | accepted | A | church, poetech | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, REALITY-TRACE, WAYS-REVIEW, APP-IS-PRIMARY, MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY |

| [DR-0299](DR-0299-the-reader-reads-the-content-and-does-not-press-the-buttons.md) | **The reader reads the content and does not press the buttons.** Darrell 2026-08-13 from the Scripture tab: *"The reader reads the Highlight Up Arrow... I want the content"* and *"the color tab pops up on its own after a while."* Two reports, ONE root cause — the reader treated interactive chrome as reading material: it SPOKE control labels and it CLICKED controls. (1) `readablePageText()` is the fallback for any surface with no registered reading, and **Scripture registered none**, so its reading was the whole of `<main>.innerText` — which carries every label: *"HIDE OTHER TRANSLATIONS · ESV · NIV · CLEAR HIGHLIGHT · GIVE · FEEDBACK"* threaded through the Word. (2) `revealForReading()` clicks every `[aria-expanded="false"]` to open collapsed content, and `VerseHighlighter`'s swatch is exactly that shape while opening a `role="menu"` — so the reader popped a palette open on EVERY verse (the two-at-once in his screenshot). `read-reveal.js`'s own header already said it must never touch menus; **the guard was right, the component never carried the attribute.** Fixed at BOTH layers on purpose — the component declares `aria-haspopup="menu"`, AND the guard refuses any button that OWNS a popup (aria-controls, or an adjacent menu/listbox/dialog) attribute-or-not, because a guard that works only if every future author remembers one attribute is a comment, not a mechanism. Third time in one day for that shape. Also: Scripture registers a real reading for the first time (gaining follow-along + hands-free `next()`), and `scripture-reading.js` composes one-press-of-play from title → lens → soul → **every authored depth, light to deep** → views → each verse as reference + KJV + gloss. The ladder is composed from DATA not the DOM: the page shows one tier at a time, so a scraper would sound fine while silently withholding Deep; `depthLadder()` avoids `resolveDepth()` because that helper falls back DOWN and would speak the same paragraph three times. ESV refused on **copyright** not capability (corpus is `public/bible/kjv` only; the ESV/NIV buttons are outbound links) — pinned in a test so nobody quietly pastes it in. **Carries a correction:** the agent told Darrell hands-free listening "needs the cloud voice path" — wrong twice; `background-audio.js` (silent looping `<audio>` + MediaSession) is already wired in `use-read-aloud.js:123`, works on Android with the DEVICE voice, and the axis is speechSynthesis-vs-audio-element, not local-vs-cloud (the clip path is SOVEREIGN FIRST → the NAS). An honest-limits header is a source to READ, not a summary to repeat. Proven-to-catch: palette fix reverted → 4/10 fail; chrome strip reverted → 2/10 fail; the still-opens-a-real-disclosure cases pass throughout. 29 new pins; suite 7,733 green. Honest remainder: the iOS gap is quoted from its module, not measured here (no iOS device) — re-review 2026-08-27; and **this DR was itself a dangling citation for ~20 minutes** (PR #1243's subject cites it, the file landed after the merge — the exact DR-0292 defect closed earlier the same day), so the real close is a gate failing any commit that cites a DR with no file — re-review 2026-08-20 | accepted | A | church | WORD-FIRST, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY, COMMUNITY-FIRST, REALITY-TRACE |

| [DR-0300](DR-0300-the-whole-world-english-bible-is-ingested-and-an-omission-is-a-fact-not-a-hole.md) | **The whole World English Bible is ingested.** Darrell 2026-08-13: *"yes ingest the WEB translation."* He had asked for the ESV; it cannot be reproduced (`bible-editions.js`: EXCLUDED, *"Copyrighted (Crossway)… never reproduce"*). That should not have ended the answer, and it nearly did — **the repo's own registry already pointed at the alternative**: WEB at `reproduce: true` with the trademark note already written, licence-verified 2026-06-25 against primary sources, and that review's open item was literally *"ingest the full WEB + KJV… beyond the curated 180-verse seed."* The KJV half shipped in July; this was the missing half, unfound because the first answer stopped at "ESV is not possible" instead of asking what was. 66 books, 31,103 verses, 4.1 MB, same per-book shape as the KJV with names taken from the KJV index so the editions cannot drift; the loader is edition-parameterized (default `'kjv'`) so a further PD edition is a folder, not a code path. Verbatim + labelled WEB because the NAME is trademarked even though the TEXT is PD. **The gate hard-failed on its first run at Luke 17:36 — a textual FACT, not a hole** (a TR-only verse the critical text omits); failing on those makes an honest critical-text edition impossible to ingest, ignoring them lets a parse regression ship disguised as divergence, so empty slots are RECORDED in the index and the total BOUNDED at 40. All five were then checked individually: four TR-only, and **Romans 16:25 is not an omission at all but a VERSIFICATION difference** — the WEB places that doxology at 14:24, verified by finding "able to establish you" there. Proven-to-catch on identity, not plausibility: John 3:16 "one and only" never "only begotten"; 1 John 5:7 without the Comma Johanneum; no thee/thou/saith; Psalm 119 not truncated (the poetry-rejoin bug, where a verse arrives as several line fragments and assigning instead of appending keeps only the last); no markup or folded-in headings. Finding for our own voice: the WEB renders the divine name **Yahweh** where the KJV prints "the LORD" (Psalm 23:1, Isaiah 42:8) — the DR-0076 bright line untouched, quotations verbatim either way. 19 pins; suite 7,752 green. Honest remainder: the corpus is on disk but **not yet surfaced** in the verse cards or the spoken reading (re-review 2026-08-20); the licence rests on our dated review because ebible.org is unreachable from the sandbox; and the npm package's `UNLICENSED` field refers to the wrapper, not the text | accepted | A | church | WORD-FIRST, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, SOVEREIGN-FIRST, COMMUNITY-FIRST, MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY |
| [DR-0301](DR-0301-read-along-reads-the-whole-core-and-a-test-that-cannot-fail-is-theatre.md) | **Read-along reads the whole core, not step 1 of 4 — and a test that cannot fail is theatre.** Darrell 2026-08-13 on Session 8 of 8: *"only part 1 of the core is taught or read by the reader… I want to hear the whole lesson and course from one play action… almost."* **The truncation was not where it looks.** The SPOKEN TEXT was never short — `readAloudTextFromArc` has always pushed `lessonPlan.segments` in full (lesson-flow.js:268). But the reader does not read that text when it can map the named ELEMENT: follow-along is alignment-by-construction, so **the DOM IS the reading**, and the DOM held one step. `showAll` already opened every ARC STAGE (which is why the later stages were visible in his screenshot while the core said "STEP 2 OF 4") — **the reveal stopped one level short**, at `AgePacedLesson`, which rendered `segments[cur]` and had no `showAll` prop at all. Decides: in read-along the whole core renders in order with its step markers kept; outside it the stepper is untouched (pacing is for a READER who turns the page, a LISTENER cannot); paging controls vanish only while reading. **The part worth keeping is the test.** Its first version PASSED and proved nothing — mounted on a Healthy Living lesson that paces to a SINGLE adult segment, so no stepper rendered, the guard matched nothing, and three assertions returned early. Caught only by distrusting the green enough to probe whether the guard fired. Retargeting to a course that does chunk STILL missed, because the screenshot is a COHORT SESSION view and which paced core mounts depends on which path renders — an assertion resting on that much scaffolding is a coin flip. So `AgePacedLesson` is exported and rendered DIRECTLY with a real four-step plan: the thing that broke is the thing under test and cannot pass by accident, with a separate case pinning that the catalog really produces multi-step cores so the fixture is not a fiction. Same failure as the bug it chased — something that looked complete while the part that mattered was absent. Proven-to-catch: showAll branch removed → 4 of 9 fail; the without-read-along case passes throughout so the reader's pacing was not flattened. 9 pins; suite 7,761 green. Honest remainder: the COURSE-boundary half is unproven (only within-lesson is pinned — Darrell's "almost" is exactly that gap), re-review 2026-08-20; and a THIRD nested pager would not be caught — the class-level close is a derived check that every component indexing into authored text accepts `showAll`, re-review 2026-08-27 | accepted | A | church | COMMUNITY-FIRST, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT |
| [DR-0302](DR-0302-the-words-being-read-are-visible-at-any-font-size.md) | **The words being read are visible — at any font size.** Darrell 2026-08-13, reading with the reader running: *"the words are blocked at the top of the highlighted text… possibly because of the banner"* and *"Account for different font sizes as well."* **Two defects in one line** (`el.scrollIntoView({block:'center'})`): it scrolled the ELEMENT not the RANGE — centring a TALL paragraph puts its opening lines above the viewport, so on exactly the long teaching paragraphs this app is built from the words being read sat off-screen while the paragraph looked correctly centred — and it knew nothing about the fixed/sticky chrome (session bar + church banner + sub-tab strip). Decides: scroll the RANGE; **MEASURE the chrome, never type it** (`stickyTopInset` probes elementsFromPoint at three x positions so a narrow floating control is not mistaken for a bar, counts only top-anchored fixed/sticky boxes so a fixed FOOTER or the GIVE button never pushes the reading down, and clamps at 45% because a chrome claiming more is an overlay whose honouring would scroll the reading OUT of view) — a constant would be wrong for most readers since the chrome changes with large print, with dismissed banners, and between the church door and the PoeTech shell; **the gap is ONE LINE of the reader's chosen size** (five sizes ship, A→A44; a fixed 24px is thinner than one line at A44, jamming the sentence against the chrome for exactly the readers needing most room) from computed line-height, falling back to 1.4× font-size when line-height is `normal` — the default in most of this app's prose — with 24px only as a floor; a well-placed sentence is LEFT ALONE so the page does not twitch every sentence; and a sentence taller than the band lands top-first, never centred, which is the original bug in miniature. **jsdom has no layout**, so a render test could only assert zeros — the very "passes while proving nothing" failure DR-0301 had just recorded — so the arithmetic is split into pure functions and tested with the rects a real browser produces. 15 pins including large print clearing by 72px where small clears by 24px; suite 7,776 green. Honest remainder: verified by arithmetic, not by eye — no screenshot of the fixed scroll on a real phone exists, and proving it the way DR-0298 proves the lesson link needs a probe case that starts a read and screenshots mid-sentence (re-review 2026-08-20); `followRange` scrolls the WINDOW so a reading inside its own scroll container would not move (none does today); and the 45% clamp is a number chosen rather than derived — the one value here that could be wrong on an untried device | accepted | A | church, poetech | COMMUNITY-FIRST, EXCELLENCE-STANDARD, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, NO-STATIC-DATA, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT |

| [DR-0303](DR-0303-the-list-never-carries-the-bytes-and-the-backend-needs-its-own-witness.md) | **Everyone was locked out of every app on an egress quota — the list never carries the bytes, and the backend needs a witness of its own.** Darrell 2026-08-13: *"Everyone got logged out of their accounts and their pins aren't working to get them back in… Christina and Shay my sister's MooreDivahs App and my wife's TLC Therapy Solutions App… also I'm no longer logged into PoeTech App"*, then *"you go to supabase and check."* **poetech.us was UP the whole time** — Cloudflare served the shell, the deploy ledger was green, site-health had nothing to report. Supabase answered **HTTP 402** on `/auth/v1/token`, `/auth/v1/signup` and every `/rest/v1` path: *"restricted due to the following violations: exceed_egress_quota"* (read from the edge logs, independently confirmed by Darrell photographing the raw string inside the app's own sign-in modal). Org on `free`. One root cause, two faces: signed out because the session could not refresh, unable to return because the PIN check is an RPC on the same restricted API. The DB was `ACTIVE_HEALTHY` and **nothing was lost** — the billing gate sits in front of the API, not the data. **The loudest suspect was wrong:** `voice_profiles` was 92 of ~120 requests (84% of traffic) but is all uuid/text/jsonb metadata — request count is not bytes. The real spend is `public.feedback`: **119 rows, 6.4 MB, of which 6.2 MB is base64 image data carried by 24 rows** (largest single value 425 kB) — and `subscribeFeedback` pulled all of it with `.select('*')`, no limit, once per SIGN-IN for EVERY signed-in user (wired into the main app shell, not an admin surface) and again in full on every realtime INSERT by anyone. Decides: the list NAMES its columns (a star silently ships the next big column someone adds) with no image column; the query is BOUNDED at 500; presence is DERIVED in the DB (migration 0135, two STORED generated columns) so the board says "3 screenshots" truthfully while moving ~8 bytes instead of ~260 kB — dropping the badge would be a lie by omission, guessing it a painted number; and the bytes move only when a person opens that card (`fetchFeedbackImages`). The money decision is Darrell's alone and the agent spent nothing. **Two defects in this record's own SQL were caught by RUNNING it against a temp table with seven row shapes, not reading it:** `false OR NULL` is NULL, so `has_screenshot` returned NULL — not false — for the 95-of-119 empty case, where the client's fallback would have computed the right answer for the wrong reason (*"nothing should happen by luck"*); and an EMPTY `screenshots` array shadowed a legacy `screenshot`, so the two columns disagreed about the same row. **The structural finding outlives the fix:** every instrument was green because not one makes an authenticated request to the backend — a 402 moves none of them, since the HTML still serves. Detection path was the principal's eyes, via three other people first; delay ~20 hours. This is **P31 one layer down** — deploy-green is not site-up; site-up is not APP-USABLE. Closed here: site-health step 7 probes `auth/v1/health` with the anon key (the path the app walks), files 402 on the incident ledger naming the violation, retries once before calling it unreachable, and NEVER reads unmeasured as healthy. Proven-to-catch on both halves: reverting `select('*')`/the limit → 2 of 15 fail; the probe run against a local 402/500/000/200/401 server behaves on every branch (and that run found a real bug — `curl` prints `000` AND the `|| echo` fired, making `000000`, so the retry never triggered). 15 pins; suite 679 files green. Honest remainder: `moore-showcase` is a PUBLIC bucket of unresized camera originals — one is 10.6 MB, three are 24 MB of its 28 MB — not today's proven driver but the next ceiling, resize-at-upload, re-review 2026-08-21; and there is no usage/billing tool on the MCP channel, so the cause is measured on the table and the code path, not read off a bill — sufficient, not proven sole | accepted | A | poetech, church, moore, tlc | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, EXECUTION-OUTCOME-OBSERVABILITY, COMMUNITY-FIRST, LESSONS-LEARNED, MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT |

| [DR-0304](DR-0304-the-reader-and-the-follow-map-read-from-one-root.md) | **The reader and the follow map read from ONE root — a page without a `<main>` was spoken perfectly and highlighted not at all.** Darrell 2026-08-14: *"this page just reads without a reader highlighting the words and following the word we currently reads… comprehensive review of the reader and pages to make sure it's working."* **Two findings, one shape.** (1) Completes DR-0299: that fix taught `readablePageText()` to strip the app's furniture, but that function is only the FALLBACK — the primary page path builds `buildFollowMap(main)` from `SKIP_SELECTOR`, a narrower list excluding `.tts-controls` and dialogs but NOT `button`/`nav`/`[role=tablist]`/`[role=menu]`. So on every page-read surface except ChurchLearn, Presenter and ScriptureLibrary the reader still spoke the furniture, and every button label also became a mapped RANGE — which is why highlights landed on chrome instead of words. The derived test extracts BOTH lists from source and asserts containment; on its first run it found a gap missed by hand (`.feedback-modal` stripped from speech, still mapped). (2) The one Darrell reported, and worse: the TEXT extractor falls back to `document.body` when a surface renders no `<main>`; the FOLLOW MAP did not — built only when `querySelector('main')` returned an element, `null` otherwise. **Only six files in this app render a `<main>`**, so on every other surface the reader spoke the page perfectly and highlighted NOTHING, because there was no map to highlight from. Three call sites carried the divergence, including `talkAbout`'s digest, which described such surfaces from `null` — a person asking "what is this?" got the help entry with none of the real on-screen numbers. Decides: one exported `readingRoot()` (`main || body || null`) that every read path calls — the thing itself, not a comment asking the next person to remember; `SKIP_SELECTOR` derived against `CHROME_SELECTOR`; and the guard is CLASS-level — any `querySelector('main')` in TTSControl outside the one definition fails the build and NAMES the line, so a fourth divergent site fails CI rather than waiting to be reported from a phone. **The recurring shape, named:** six instances this week of *two places that must agree, kept in agreement by a comment* — `getInitialChurchView` vs `parseNav`; `VALID_VIEWS` vs the shell's `VALID`; the read-reveal guard vs an ARIA attribute; `PRESERVED_PARAMS` vs what the app reads; `CHROME_SELECTOR` vs `SKIP_SELECTOR`; the text root vs the map root. Every one shipped green; every one was found by a person using the app. The durable answer is derive-one-from-the-other or both-call-one-function, with a test that fails on drift. Proven-to-catch: restoring `querySelector('main') || null` fails the derived case and names the offending line; removing the body fallback fails the behavioural cases. 7 pins. Honest remainder: verified in jsdom, not on a phone — these prove the map is BUILT and both halves agree on the root, not that the highlight is visible on a real device (DR-0302's probe-that-screenshots-mid-read is still open, re-review 2026-08-20); and **the comprehensive review is NOT finished** — the remaining surfaces have not each been walked as a user (dimension 2) and the form-factor sweep (dimension 4) has not run on the reader, re-review 2026-08-15 | accepted | A | church, poetech | COMMUNITY-FIRST, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY, EXCELLENCE-STANDARD, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT |

| [DR-0305](DR-0305-the-audit-log-refreshes-by-delta-and-egress-has-a-witness.md) | **The audit log refreshes by delta; the front door was 402 while the database was healthy.** Darrell 2026-08-14 with five screenshots: *"No matter how I try I can't reach or login to my admin account... and other users as well"* then *"notices the words on the error..."* Supabase had **hard-restricted the project** — `exceed_egress_quota` — so the **API gateway** 402'd while `list_projects` read `ACTIVE_HEALTHY` and the database answered in milliseconds over the direct pooler. The org is on the **free** plan (5 GB egress). `record_events` is the generic audit log: **20,129 rows, 15 MB, largest single row 1.18 MB** of before/after JSON, and `createTableSync.subscribe()` called `fetchAll()` on **every realtime change** — ~340 such refetches spend the whole monthly budget. The sharpest part: the pagination loop inside `fetchAll` was added as a **correctness** fix (a plain `.select()` truncates at 1,000 rows, which had silently dropped half of Christina's transaction ledger) — so a correctness fix made the egress bill strictly worse, and **nothing measured egress**, so it stayed invisible until service was cut. `createTableSync` gains an opt-in `appendOnly` delta: first load reads the whole table, later refreshes fetch only rows newer than the newest held. Sound for `record_events` **specifically** because its merge is a union and the DB grants SELECT+INSERT only (migration 0052) — a row cannot change after it is written. Cheaper never means less correct: a failed delta falls back to the FULL read, a **partial** read does not advance the watermark, `gt` not `gte`, identical paging, tenant-scoped throughout. 9 tests, verified by regression. **NOT closed:** `transactions` is the second burner (2,953 rows / 3.4 MB / **17,374 lifetime writes**) and takes updates+deletes so `appendOnly` is unsound there — it is the books ledger that already carries the scar above, `re-review: 2026-08-21`; and *"Please try again in a little while"* is a **false promise** for a quota stop that never self-heals, `re-review: 2026-08-21` | accepted | A | all | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, COST-DISCIPLINE, DATA-DRIVEN-LIVING, SURFACE-PREMISE, DECISION-RECORDS |

| [DR-0306](DR-0306-the-sovereign-supabase-stack-and-a-migration-that-proves-itself.md) | **The sovereign Supabase stack, and a migration that proves itself.** Darrell 2026-08-14: *"I'm not paying them... other options!!!!!!!?"* → *"start the NAS supabase stack."* Self-hosted Supabase serves the **identical API** — measured **196 `.from()` · 68 `.auth` · 64 `.rpc()` · 3 `.storage` · 2 `.channel`**, all 333 unchanged — so cutover is a URL + anon key, which is exactly what beats moving to another free Postgres host where those 68 auth sites would need rebuilding against something that is not GoTrue; RLS stays enforced by Postgres so DR-0060 carries over. **The measurement that decided the strategy:** 183 tables, **1,719 RLS policies**, 110 functions, 91 triggers, 23 users, **50 MB**, 3 buckets/455 objects — and the 1,719 is mostly GENERATED (`viewer_readonly_*` × 159 = 477, `assistant_scope_*` × 158 = 632, ~610 hand-written), produced by DO-loops in `migrations-auto/`, which is why grep finds 495 `CREATE POLICY` where Postgres reports 1,719. **So the repo's own migration history reproduces the schema** — no dump; replay migrations (DR-0084's lane), copy 50 MB, verify. Eight containers, **every port `127.0.0.1` only** (8800/5433/8801, clear of the twelve measured live), **nothing points at it** so a half-proven stack can never take the app down.
