| [DR-0316](DR-0316-the-rate-card-is-the-teams-and-a-held-deposit-is-not-income.md) | **The rate card is the team's, and a held deposit is never income.** Christina, Director of Ministries for The Love Corner, 2026-08-30, submitting the Commercial Event Facility Rental Proposal — the first real dollar rates the platform has carried for the church's own spaces ($1,000/hr facility; $50/hr per sound person, 2-4 typical; $35/hr per security person, 5-10 typical; $500 cleaning; $1,000 refundable deposit; 50% of the FACILITY RENTAL at signing, balance 30 days out, $0 on the day) — and, in the same breath, the part that decided the architecture: *"this will need to be able to be updated based on what the whole team and staff would like to see... a great opportunity for default settings to be able to be discussed... inside the Love Corner App."* So her numbers are the SEED, not the law: `DEFAULT_COMMERCIAL_RATE_CARD` in code, `venue_rate_cards` holding only what the team CHANGED, `mergeRateCard()` the one seam every surface reads through (an override can never blank a rate by omission), plus `venue_rate_card_notes` — the discussion append-only beside the numbers. Status is DATA (`proposed` until the team moves it; a bogus status can never read as approved), because a proposal rendered as a settled price is the painted-number failure where it costs most. And a held deposit is NEVER income: the refundable deposit is carried apart from `eventCharges` all the way through, so only real charges reach `quoted_price`. Quotes store INPUTS, never totals, so an agreed rate change reprices every open booking. Closes the "staff-editable rate card is a documented follow-up" note venue-rental.js has carried since the booking table shipped. PROVEN-TO-CATCH on three mutations (deposit-as-income, bogus-status-as-approved, signing-share-on-the-total); the test-writing itself caught a real bug — absent staff hours stored as 0, silently dropping sound and security from every saved quote. Open: no per-campus tier multiplier (her proposal quotes one facility rate) — re-review: 2026-09-30 | accepted | B | Church > Venues; the Rate card tab; migration 0162 | DR-0076, DR-0065, DR-0061, DR-0075, DR-0074 |
| [DR-0315](DR-0315-the-law-binds-at-aa-and-destruction-goes-through-one-door.md) | **The law binds at AA, and destruction goes through one door.** Darrell 2026-08-29: "Research the best UIUX standard for accessibility because the US government has laws that fine those who dont comply" — and "solutions?!!!" on the destructive-confirm refusal. RESEARCHED, not recalled: the enforceable tier is WCAG **Level AA** everywhere the law speaks — DOJ ADA Title II final rule (28 CFR 35, WCAG 2.1 AA; deadlines extended to Apr 26 2027/2028), Title III courts de facto (3,117 federal suits in 2025), EAA; target size at AA is **2.5.8 = 24×24px**, while 44×44 is **2.5.5 AAA** + platform HIG. So the recorded 44-vs-36 conflict DISSOLVES: 24 legal floor / 36 enforced house floor (1.5× the law) / 44 AAA aim — nothing lowered; house posture = WCAG 2.2 AA (the UIUC campus standard). And the "not statically gateable" destructive-confirm limit fell to DR-0131: `confirmThen()` in lib/confirm-action.js is the ONE door destruction goes through, making the question one import a scan CAN answer — the gate's first run caught SIX live unguarded destructions (recipe, song idea, budget goal, calendar ×3, cloud rows included), all fixed same-day, so the kind locks HARD at zero with REVERSIBLE_BY_DESIGN naming the one exemption. Bonus: resolving focus-carrying constants healed 93 never-owed baseline entries (341→248, zero added, set-diff proven). Ungated WCAG 2.2 AA criteria inventoried with dates — 3.3.8 accessible-authentication (the PIN gate) re-review 2026-09-15 | accepted | B | every interactive control; Pattern 2g; ui-standards-guard | DR-0314, DR-0131, DR-0076, DR-0100, DR-0075, DR-0259 |
| [DR-0314](DR-0314-a-standard-that-lives-only-in-code-is-a-coincidence.md) | **A standard that lives only in implementations is a coincidence — the review that finds one lands it as a Way AND a gate.** Darrell 2026-08-28, on the Properties picture form: "we have multiple pictures upload etc... all these features need to be applied as we build without needing to keep saying it... our standards are higher than this build" — and the harder question, "claude keeps lying about reviewing our Ways... how can that be fixed too". MEASURED: `multiple` was already on 5 image pickers, add-in-place already on several dropdowns, disabled-says-why on most controls — and NONE of the three was written down anywhere in docs/. So an honest, complete review of every Way would still have missed them; the standards existed as implementations and zero rules. Decision: the review that discovers a standard must land it in THREE places in the same delivery — the Way (UX-PATTERNS Pattern 2f), the GATE (reads real source, written BEFORE the fix and observed failing on the real defect, DR-0076 §3), and a named exception list WITH reasons (SINGULAR_BY_DESIGN — a receipt is one receipt; a guard that fires where it should not is noise, and noise is how a guard gets deleted). Why "review harder" is the wrong fix: three times in one day a met standard was dropped in new code, each found on his phone, each because the standard was not machinery; and the picture form passed all 9,514 tests while taking one file — the blind spot that writes the bug writes the test, so a test written after the fact tests what was already understood. Shipped: Pattern 2f, ui-standards-guard.test.js (proven-to-catch on all three), GalleryTab multi-picture queue + `+ Add a room…` + honest disabled reason. Carried: the rest of the UI standard set, one pattern + one gate per class — re-review: 2026-09-11 | accepted | B | every form the app ships | MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, APP-IS-PRIMARY, QUALITY-OF-LIFE |

| [DR-0317](DR-0317-the-repoint-moved-the-rows-not-the-blobs.md) | **The repoint moved the rows, not the blobs — a named NOT-done is a scheduled outage of whatever reads across it.** Darrell 2026-08-31: "What happened to the images for our MooreDivahs App?!!!!!!" — Shay's front screen showed her twelve pieces as empty boxes, titles and prices perfect. The reported diagnosis (a dropped getPublicUrl transform, or a cache-only service worker) was wrong on both counts, and its decisive evidence was inverted: ZERO browser requests in the hosted project's edge logs is not a symptom, it is REPOINT-ARMED (2026-08-19, DR-0310) working — the app had been building against https://poetech.us/sb for twelve days and had stopped calling the hosted project at all. MEASURED: showcase.js still called getPublicUrl (never dropped); sw.js routes images down `cached || fetch(req)` (no cache-only path); kong.yml routes /sb/storage/v1/ to a sovereign storage with no moore-showcase bucket. Root cause: cutover_sync.py names "3 buckets / 455 objects" NOT copied, on purpose — the rows came across, the blobs did not, so every getPublicUrl built a correct URL at a backend that does not hold the file. Shipped: VITE_PUBLIC_STORAGE_URL bridge (public blobs resolve where they live while everything else stays sovereign; one workflow line is the whole retirement), migration 0163 (the SELECT policy 0092 never wrote — the bucket works today only because public=true bypasses RLS, a silent fail-closed trap), compress-at-upload in addPiece (10.6MB originals into 180px thumbnails; a transform URL is the wrong fix — the sovereign stack runs no imgproxy), and tests proven-to-catch all three. Then the harder half, found by asking who ELSE reads across the gap: the two PRIVATE buckets were just as broken and nobody reported it — church-team-documents (322 objects, Christina's 184 team-document rows, EVERY one a Storage path) and sermon-documents (121 objects) have failed to open since 2026-08-19, because a signed URL must be minted by the backend that HOLDS the file and the user's session is now a sovereign JWT the hosted RLS refuses. A private bucket cannot be bridged; the bytes must move. So storage_sync.py was BUILT this session (all 455 objects, idempotent + resumable, per-bucket parity where an empty bucket can never read GO, pure logic gated in CI and observed failing on two real defects first). Remaining ask is one value only Darrell holds: the hosted service_role key in agent.env. Carried: the blob copy run itself, and the backfill of the twelve oversized originals — re-review: 2026-09-30 | accepted | B | Moore Divahs front screen, every public bucket | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, APP-IS-PRIMARY, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY |

| [DR-0319](DR-0319-a-health-log-is-hers-alone-and-no-number-in-it-is-invented.md) | **A health log is hers alone, and no number in it is invented.** Darrell 2026-08-30/31, building his wife's weight-loss program: "PLANNED DATA and ACTUAL DATA must remain separate", "I can't put my food in here and I need to", "find it from some data base online... keep it stored", and "you create a tracking plan based on the pdf my wife gave you!!!!!!!!! obviously!!!!!!!". Four parts: (1) OWNER-SCOPED, a deliberate divergence from the family-pooled pattern — 0164/0165/0166 scope every row to `created_by = auth.uid()`, no member/admin/anon policy, so an instance owner cannot read her weigh-ins; the first cut gated the ROWS and left the TAB ungated, which `canSeeHealthTab()` closes (RLS was never the hole, the nav was); (2) planned/actual cannot merge STRUCTURALLY — there is no setter for a planned value anywhere in health-actions.js; (3) NO NUMBER IS INVENTED — no seeded nutrition (sandbox proxy refuses CONNECT, 403), a blank is ABSENT not zero, the lookup returns a number only when a database returned one, her confirmed values beat the API, the food-name vocabulary carries no nutrition, and the juice servings are DERIVED from his verified 18.3 cal/oz rather than typed; (4) history is immutable because nutrition is stored on the entry at log time. The plan itself was then built from his wife's own document — strength round, 28-min walk, nineteen foods and the 1,604/141.7 daily totals verbatim — while the per-food calorie table she never wrote stays absent. Gates caught four real bugs first: a fill colour used as text (2.46:1, invisible in midnight), `Number(null)`→0 charting a 0 lb weigh-in, `oz \|\| default` turning an explicit 0 into 18, and an unconfirmed destructive delete. Shell mount cut +11→+5, budget raised 5331→5335 with itemisation; every later extension cost zero shell lines. | [DR-0320](DR-0320-what-makes-having-you-better-is-a-covenant-question-not-a-contract-one.md) | **"What makes having you better?" is a covenant question, not a contract one.** Darrell 2026-09-02 pasted a recorded exchange (Darwynn McPherson, via Facebook) with one word: "lesson." Two men argue about a household — if I pay every bill, why would I cook and clean — and end on: if she can already survive without your paycheck, what makes having you in her life better? Shipped as Living Lessons **L114** (eleven movements, all three age bands, 6-question quiz, facilitator guide). Five decisions: (1) FIX THE FRAME FIRST — every question in the clip is a CONTRACT question and Scripture frames marriage as a covenant with a Witness (Malachi 2:14) between one flesh (Genesis 2:24), so the chore argument is answered from covenant, not from the market; (2) BOTH MONEY TIERS IN ONE BREATH (DR-0100) — provision is commanded, heavy and honourable (1 Timothy 5:8; Genesis 3:19; James 2:15-16), stated plainly and never shamed, AND it does not repeal 1 Peter 3:7; the reverse over-reach ("money does not matter") dies on the same page; the clip's causal politics is flagged NARROWLY as genuinely contested with a stated reason it need not be settled; (3) THE TOWEL SETTLES THE CHORES QUESTION (John 13:4-5,14-15; Mark 10:43-45; 1 Peter 5:3) — if serving in a house unmanned anyone it would have unmanned Him; headship is responsibility FOR the house, not exemption from it, and the ledger is replaced by Philippians 2:3-4 (am I making her life easier), not by 50/50 scorekeeping; (4) LEVERAGE IS TREACHERY WITH THE WITNESS PRESENT (Malachi 2:14-16; Ephesians 6:9) — a hold that depends on her inability to leave is a hostage, not a covenant, which reframes her independence as EXPOSURE, not theft, while Proverbs 31:11,16,23,28 keeps an earning wife the Word's own picture of a blessing; (5) MUTUAL OR IT IS A TALKING POINT (1 Corinthians 7:3-4; Proverbs 14:1; 31:12,27; Ephesians 5:33), closing on Genesis 2:18 + Ecclesiastes 4:9-12 — the first problem was ALONE. Verified: 79 quoted spans letter-for-letter KJV from the in-repo corpus + 49 fragment pins; the whole-span gate caught ONE real in-quote alteration (a comma pulled inside "the wife of thy covenant,") and SIXTEEN places where the clip's words or our own prose wore Scripture's quotation marks — all fixed, so a double quote in this lesson means Scripture and nothing else; the gate also caught a real content gap (the senior band was missing the end-of-ledger movement) and the fix went to the lesson, not the test. Lint clean; 796 files / 10,435 tests passing; build clean. Carried: the stale `weeks:` enumeration comment (correct count is gated; the L99-L114 backfill is cosmetic) — re-review: 2026-10-02 | accepted | A | Living Lessons L114 — the Learn series | WORD-FIRST, THREE-TIER-HONESTY, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, TRUST-BUT-VERIFY, APP-IS-PRIMARY, RELIGION-AND-RELATIONSHIP |

| [DR-0321](DR-0321-principles-travel-with-the-money-or-the-money-leaves.md) | **Principles travel with the money, or the money leaves.** Darrell 2026-09-02 spoke the three provisions written into the family trust — the family constitution the trust POINTS AT ("if you pass down money without passing down principles, there's a good chance the wealth disappears by the second or even third generation"), the SPENDTHRIFT provision ("a lot of families think the biggest threat to generational wealth is taxes. Sometimes it's not... lawsuits, creditors, bad business decisions, divorce, or even the wrong marriage... because the assets remain owned by the trust, not them individually"), and FORCED INCOME PRODUCTION ("our beneficiaries don't just take distributions, they also learn how to produce, build, invest, and contribute value back into it") — with the build order: "lesson.. and business system built into PoeTech App right now... prepopulate it for our family... and users will be able to educate theirs." The teaching arrived attached to a FUNNEL (a countdown timer over a "free LIVE masterclass," a seat costing an email address, an upsell at the end): the substance is sound, the delivery is a toll booth in front of what a household cannot afford to skip — so it is built in whole and free. Four parts: (1) THE LESSON — `legacy-provisions-course.js`, six self-paced lessons registered in LEARN_CATALOG (built-but-unsurfaced is not shipped, DR-0318's class), both age bands, quizzes, a DECLARED `meta.wordFirst` rather than a borrowed anchor (DR-0282); it pairs with Handed Forward instead of duplicating it — that course forms the HEIR, this teaches the STRUCTURE the heir inherits; 21 KJV fragments verbatim from the in-repo corpus and pinned, the gate OBSERVED FAILING on a one-word drift ("Occupy till I come" → "until") before it was trusted. (2) THE SYSTEM — `family-trust.js` splits the 0052-recipes / Road-to-150 way: the AUTHORED half (ten prepopulated constitution articles, the three provision records with drafting checklists and honest limits, the production policy, ten spendthrift review questions) ships as version-controlled code so the canonical family document can never be lost; migration `0167` (`family_trust_records`) holds only what a family actually DID. Scoped to the INSTANCE, a deliberate divergence from DR-0319: a weigh-in is one person's, but a trustee reading a beneficiary's standing IS the mechanism of the third provision. (3) THE HONESTY IS MACHINERY — painting the engine two ways (summing absent amounts as zero; letting an empty ledger read as a pass) turns TEN tests red: an empty ledger reports no-record with a NULL total (never zero, never a pass), a check with missing inputs reports unknown (never pass), `distributionReview` NEVER clears on unknown or no-record (it fails safe toward the governor, not toward the money), an unanswered spendthrift item reads unreviewed (never protected) and one exposure keeps the whole posture unconfirmed, an answer past the review interval goes stale, and an attestation does not manufacture a production standing. (4) THE LIMITS RIDE IN THE DATA — self-settled trusts, child support and certain government claims, funds already received — so every surface renders what the clause does NOT do WITH the provision. Teaching and a family operating system, never legal advice. Mounted inside Books → Plan (the shell sits exactly at its frozen 5,335-line budget) at a cost of ZERO shell lines. Found on the way and fixed for every sync: `table-sync`'s subscribe IIFE was un-awaited and un-caught, so a failure became an unhandled rejection instead of a logged, best-effort miss. Asked mid-build ("also the support system for the PoeTech App?") and shipped in the same delivery: the Plan tab had NO Help entry at all before this — the two-tier layer now covers it (light about in `surface-help.js`, deep `books:plan` entry in `help-content.js`, reconciled by `help-freshness --sync`), beside the course's Ari tutor and the "I want help with mine" interest capture. Carried: the in-app adopt-for-another-family flow is copy-to-clipboard today (`adoptConstitution` exists and is tested) and a roster row of its own — re-review: 2026-10-02 | accepted | B | the family trust's three provisions — Church → Learn and Books → Plan | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, NO-STATIC-DATA, REALITY-TRACE, APP-IS-PRIMARY, SPOKEN-TEACHINGS-ARE-BUILD-INPUT, COMMUNITY-FIRST, WORD-FIRST |

| [DR-0322](DR-0322-the-migration-never-ran-because-docker-was-not-on-the-path.md) | **The migration never ran because docker was not on the PATH — and the standard that would have caught it lived only in code.** DR-0321 merged as `e4982c6` carrying migration `0167`; the deploy proved out (build, deploy and the real-browser boot check all green on that SHA) and **db-migrate went red**: `LEDGER-BEFORE=151 ... sudo: docker: command not found ... LEDGER-AFTER=151`. Zero migrations applied — not `0167` failing on its SQL, NOTHING ran, which is why the ledger did not move and why the lane correctly refused to call itself green (DR-0317 doing its job). ROOT CAUSE: `replay_migrations.sh` opened `DOCKER="docker"; docker ps || DOCKER="sudo -n docker"` — and DSM does not put docker on the PATH of a NON-LOGIN shell, which is exactly what an ssh command gets, so the probe failed command-not-found and the sudo fallback failed identically (sudo resolves against the same PATH); `set -e` killed it at the first psql call. `e4982c6` was simply the FIRST migration to ride the sovereign replay lane wired in the day before (PR #1426, `0b9eaea`) — the defect was latent from that moment. THE FINDING THAT MATTERS MORE THAN THE FIX: the standard already existed IN CODE ONLY — this script's own caller, `sovereign-replay-over-tailnet.sh`, resolves the binary correctly and even carries the comment "DSM does not put docker on the PATH", and ten `nas-update-*.sh` scripts use absolute paths — while NOTHING in `docs/` said to. DR-0314's exact class, arriving on schedule: a wrapper already burned by this failure called a sibling that had not been. So it lands in DR-0314's three places: (1) THE WAY — `INFRASTRUCTURE-PIPELINE.md` gains a *NAS execution conventions* section with the exact idiom, the reference implementations, and the incident, sitting beside DR-0272's two conventions which had no shared home; (2) THE GATE — `scripts/nas-docker-path-guard.mjs` reads real shell sources, strips comments (prose saying "docker pulls" is not an offender — noise is how a guard gets deleted), and was WRITTEN BEFORE THE FIX AND OBSERVED FAILING on `replay_migrations.sh:30` as it stood at `e4982c6`, re-proved in the unit test against that exact original source; it also found what a hand-search missed — `choir_dates_install.sh:64` carried the IDENTICAL probe-and-fallback shape, fixed here because a class fix that leaves a known sibling broken is not a class fix; (3) A NAMED EXCEPTION WITH ITS REASON — `pull-deepseek-r1.sh` does not ASSUME the PATH (its pre-flight `command -v docker` fails loudly, and it is a hand-run operator script), with the honest consequence carried rather than hidden: on DSM it therefore refuses to run over a non-login ssh shell — re-review: 2026-10-02. A FOURTH PART found while planning the proof: `db-migrate` fires on push only for `migrations-auto/**`, `db-migrate-apply.sh` or its own file — the REPLAY SCRIPTS were not trigger paths, so the fix for a lane that applied NOTHING would not have re-run the lane that proves it, and landing it would have needed a human to remember to dispatch. Both replay scripts are trigger paths now, by the same reasoning that already put `db-migrate-apply.sh` there; a useful side effect is that this change touches them, so its own merge fires the lane and the fix proves itself. NOT CLAIMED: the fix is in the repo, NOT yet proven on the NAS — the proof is a db-migrate run reaching `LEDGER-AFTER=152` with `0167` in `_sovereign_replay`. Until then `family_trust_records` does not exist on the sovereign database and the Legacy Provisions surface runs device-local only (its designed offline behavior — no outage, no data loss). Carried: prove the lane on the NAS; the remaining NAS convention set, one convention + one gate per class — re-review: 2026-10-02 | accepted | B | the NAS execution path — every script that runs docker there | MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, EXECUTION-OUTCOME-OBSERVABILITY, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT |

Eaton 1875, Broadway Nat'l Bank v. Adams 1882, Claflin 1889, the 1986 GST tax driving the perpetuities repeal (South Dakota first, 1983), Alaska 1997 beating the Cook Islands. Naming only the cruder man would have made the lesson the thing it corrects. | [DR-0327](DR-0327-the-witness-watched-the-wrong-backend-and-an-error-page-spoke-for-us.md) | **Christina could not sign in — the witness was watching a backend the app had stopped calling, and an error page spoke in our place.** Darrell 2026-09-03: *"My wife is locked out!!!! Why???!!!!"* Her phone showed `Unexpected token '<', "<!DOCTYPE "... is not valid JSON` under "Welcome back". MEASURED, not inferred: the NAS left the tailnet between 05:09 and 09:28 UTC — `/sb/auth/v1/health` **200** at 05:09:40 (site-health 33717706745), **525** at 09:45:22 (33740584429), and SSH with the real key `Connection timed out` twice (nas-health 33747092767 at 10:58:18; the deploy's own key fetch 33748885543 at 11:18:24). Cloudflare could not complete TLS to the Funnel, so every sign-in call returned Cloudflare's HTML 525 page; supabase-js parses every response as JSON, and the PARSER'S THROW is what she read. Her account was never involved, and no door worked — phone+PIN, password and email all ride `/sb`. **TWO DEFECTS.** (1) THE WITNESS WATCHED THE WRONG BACKEND: site-health ran 17 minutes after she was locked out, measured 525 on `/sb`, `/nas-photos` AND `/property-history`, and wrote **"UP. Fresh."** — because step 7 probes the origin named by the CI SECRETS (still hosted, answering 200 throughout) while the served app has called `/sb` since the 2026-08-19 repoint. DR-0310 §5 promised that line would be *"observe-only until /sb is live, incident-armed after"*; /sb went live 2026-08-19 and **the arming never landed — fifteen days in which the app's real backend could fail without moving the instrument**. Now armed, and armed against the SERVED BUNDLE (new step 3b reads which backend the shipped JavaScript names), so the witness follows the app instead of the configuration; an unreadable bundle arms it too (DR-0076 — unknown never reads healthy). (2) AN ERROR PAGE SPOKE IN OUR PLACE: HTML-where-JSON-belonged was an unclassified failure class, so the raw parser complaint fell through to the reader — DR-0303's defect in new clothes, against the same person. Now classified with Cloudflare's 52x family and the proxy's "upstream unreachable"; the raw string survives as `detail`. Proven-to-catch: the pins fail if any pattern or code is removed — written against four engines' wordings, and **Firefox's was missed by the first patch and caught by the test before it shipped**; the armed gate fires on today's exact readings `(served=/sb,525)`, `(unreadable,525)`, `(/sb,000)` and stays green on 200/401 and on a hosted-backend build. **THE METHOD WAS CORRECTED TWICE MID-SESSION, and both belong in the record.** The ari-guard stop-hook blocked one reply for `re-ask-permission` ("say the word") and a second for `cited-but-unread` — DR-0089, DR-0107 and DR-0317 were cited from summary and had never been opened this session. Reading them changed three things: DR-0107 §2 means this PR is **merged and UNDELIVERED**, not done (its deploy failed by design at the sovereign key fetch, DR-0310 §2, so the fix cannot reach her until the NAS answers); DR-0317 is not a reconciliation-debt example as claimed but an UNWATCHED-GAP one, which supports the arming more strongly than the point made; and DR-0089's brakes are narrower than paraphrased. Darrell then asked *"did you test with your access? cli.... ssh?"* — measured and answered: this sandbox has **no ssh binary, no tailscale, no key, and the egress proxy rejects poetech.us**, so SSH was exercised where the key lives (the runner, per DR-0108's whole-team reach), twice, independently. **EXPLICITLY NOT DECIDED:** whether to revert `infra/nas-supabase/REPOINT-ARMED`. It would restore sign-in AND unblock all deploys in one merge, but it moves where the family's data lives — hosted holds the 2026-08-19 baseline, so two weeks of rows go invisible and new writes split across two backends. Left with the Governor, held ready, not fired | accepted | A | the sign-in door, site-health, and the sovereign transport | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, EXECUTION-OUTCOME-OBSERVABILITY, COMMUNITY-FIRST, LESSONS-LEARNED, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, SOVEREIGN-FIRST |

| [DR-0328](DR-0328-a-frozen-tab-must-not-cost-every-read-five-seconds.md) | **A frozen tab must not cost every read five seconds — the acquire window fits inside the read budget, and the fourth bound was the signal.** Hours after the morning's outage was fixed, on the just-deployed build 3822a4e, Darrell's Properties card showed **four identical `not-reached` lines** while the NAS was MEASURED healthy (`supabase-auth` healthy; `supabase-rest` connected to PostgreSQL 15.8 with its schema cache loaded 13:15:14; the database returning transactions=3088, study_entries=45; RLS proofs passing) — and then one line from him settled it: *"it worked in incognito."* Isolated storage, no sibling poetech.us tab. `not-reached` is `boundedRead`'s TIMEOUT fallback, never something the database said, so the requests were not being answered from HIS BROWSER: every PostgREST call routes through `getSession()`, which serialises on a cross-tab Web Lock, Chrome FREEZES background tabs, and this family runs ~35 across three doors on one origin. **THE ARITHMETIC WAS THE BUG.** auth-js 2.106 already recovers — on its acquire timeout it STEALS the orphaned lock (supabase/supabase#42505), freeing it for everyone, so the cost is paid once — but the DEFAULT WINDOW is 5000ms while `bounded-read` gives a whole read 6000ms: a contended read spent ~83% of its budget waiting to start and reported `not-reached` having never been given a chance to answer. Fixed with the vendor's own knob, `lockAcquireTimeout: 1200`. **A HAND-ROLLED LOCK WAS WRITTEN FIRST AND DELETED BEFORE IT SHIPPED** — reading the real library source (rather than assuming the default was a plain forever-wait) showed the steal-recovery already existed, and stealing is strictly better than the custom fallback's "proceed unlocked": it RELEASES the orphan instead of routing around it, and it is the vendor's tested path in the app's most sensitive code. **THE FOURTH BOUND WAS THE SIGNAL (P49):** the boot gate (2026-07-13), Admin access+usage (2026-07-22), PIN reads, and Properties (2026-09-01) had each bounded this same lock's symptom; a bound can only choose HOW a read fails, and four honest timeouts is still four timeouts. When a workaround reaches a fourth surface, the cause is upstream and the workaround is the finding. Gated on the RELATIONSHIP, not the number: `auth-lock-window.test.js` reads BOTH real constants and was observed to fail on 5000 (the default), on 0 (fail-immediately), on -1 (wait-forever — the vendor's own documented deadlock), and on the line's removal; a floor of >=500ms is pinned too, because stealing from a genuinely live refresher would cause the token-rotation race the lock exists to prevent — a WORSE failure than the one being fixed. `bounded-read` stays as the backstop and the test pins that it does. **NOT CLAIMED:** verified by test and by reading the library's source, NOT yet by Darrell reproducing his 35-tab window on the shipped build — that live confirmation is the remaining proof (DR-0104) | accepted | A | the auth client, and every read in every surface | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, EXECUTION-OUTCOME-OBSERVABILITY, COMMUNITY-FIRST, LESSONS-LEARNED, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT |

| [DR-0329](DR-0329-the-four-legal-categories-become-real-shelves.md) | **The four legal categories become real shelves — upload or pointer, privilege decided, and the encryption we do NOT have is said out loud.** Darrell 2026-09-06 on Books → Legal: "I need a section that I can upload legal documents for each of these categories." Those four category boxes were hardcoded `<ul>` lists — orientation copy painted over nothing, the P15 class on the one surface whose entire value is trust. LEGAL-PRIVACY-BOUNDARY binds documents as "pointers only, not file content" (a considered 2026-05-18 choice), so the conflict was surfaced before any code: the resolution keeps BOTH — a FILE (bytes in the private `legal-documents` bucket, 5-minute signed URLs) or a POINTER (no bytes, where the paper actually is; works signed out and offline, first-class not fallback). RLS is CREATOR-scoped, a deliberate divergence from DR-0321: a trustee must read a beneficiary's standing, but a household member must not read another's will. `privileged` is NOT NULL and starts UNDECIDED — the form refuses `null`, because a pre-ticked default produces rows nobody decided and that defeats the export guarantee for the whole matter. All four storage verbs are owner-only (a read policy alone leaves anyone able to overwrite a will in place). The AES-GCM-256 layer is NOT built and cannot be as specified — pin.js keeps the PIN out of the browser, so no client-side key exists — and the screen says so in words: "a private shelf, not a safe deposit box." 26 tests, every refusal proven-to-catch; the suite caught a painted `0 B` for unknown sizes in the engine before merge. | accepted | B | poetech | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, REALITY-TRACE, DATA-AS-EMPOWERMENT, SOVEREIGN-FIRST, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, SURFACE-PREMISE |

| [DR-0331](DR-0331-spoken-input-is-captured-for-meaning-not-transcribed-with-its-typos.md) | **Spoken input is captured for MEANING, not transcribed with its typos — and left raw wherever the raw form already works.** Darrell 2026-09-06, mid-build of L128: *"my writing is supposed to be fixed also to reflect the meaning not my typos... unless it works... without fixing... does that make sense to you?"* Layer 0 already binds the agent to CAPTURE his spoken teachings; what was never settled is how his words are rendered when QUOTED BACK. Speaking raw from a phone produces two artifacts that are not part of what he said — typos and spoken-grammar slips (`loke` for like; `studies` where the sense is studied; `because of His Ways are keeping me protected`) and truncations (a dangling `if...`). Quoting those verbatim is fidelity to the KEYBOARD, not to the SPEAKER: it attributes to him a sentence he did not say and would not defend. Five parts: (1) FIX THE ARTIFACT, KEEP THE VOICE — typos, slips and truncations corrected; vocabulary, cadence, emphasis, capitalisation and framing never; (2) UNLESS IT WORKS WITHOUT FIXING, which is the operative half and not a tiebreaker — fragments, run-ons, all-caps emphasis and `etc...` as a real gesture all stay, and the default in doubt is leave it alone; (3) NEVER CHANGE THE CLAIM — grammar and transcription repair ONLY, with no softening, sharpening, completing an unfinished thought, or resolving an ambiguity in the agent's preferred direction; where a truncation hides something load-bearing the rule produces a QUESTION, not a guess; (4) SAY WHICH SPANS WERE CLEANED, in the artifact itself; (5) **THIS IS NOT THE SCRIPTURE RULE AND NEVER TOUCHES IT** — DR-0076/DR-0210 stay absolute, quoted Scripture is verbatim down to a typographic apostrophe, and the two live in the same lesson, which is why his spoken spans ride an explicit `NOT_SCRIPTURE` allowlist rather than a silent exemption. Worked example shipped the same session: L128 cleaned three spans and left six exactly as spoken, with the rule in the gate header, the nine spans allowlisted, and the gate additionally asserting the allowlist is HONEST (no entry secretly Scripture) and not a DUMPING GROUND (every entry appears in the lesson). Written rather than offered: the ari-guard stop-hook blocked a reply for `re-ask-permission` ("say the word and I'll make it a DR") on work Darrell had already directed by declaring the rule — DR-0111. **FOLDED INTO LAYER 0 the same session** — one short paragraph beneath "Spoken Teachings Are Build Input" in `CLAUDE.md`, pointing here rather than restating (DR-0245 fold discipline); no open item remains. **Renumbered on merge from a provisional DR-0329 per DR-0052** — PR #1453 landed DR-0329 and DR-0330 on main first | accepted | n/a | all — every artifact quoting Darrell | WORD-FIRST, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, TRUST-BUT-VERIFY, EXCELLENCE-STANDARD, DECISION-RECORDS |
| [DR-0332](DR-0332-a-count-is-not-the-question-the-replay-inverted.md) | **A count is not the question — the sovereign replay called an up-to-date database "BEHIND", and my own renumber is what tripped it.** DR-0330's follow-up renamed `0168-legal-document-shelves.sql` to `0169-…` to dodge an ordinal collision, and said renumbering was "the whole fix." That was OVERSTATED: two databases were collapsed into one. The collision was the HOSTED database's ordinal guard; the SOVEREIGN ledger is keyed by FILENAME, had already applied and ledgered `0168-…` cleanly, and was GREEN at 182/182 (runs 445, 446). The rename made the next replay apply the file again under its new name, leaving the old row — 183 rows against 182 files — and the lane failed with `the app's database is BEHIND the repo`, the exact opposite of the truth. The real defect predates the rename: success was `[ "$DONE_NOW" = "$TOTAL" ]`, a COUNT compared to a COUNT, which is a proxy for "is every file applied?" that INVERTS on an ordinary rename — and whose failure message states the opposite with total confidence, in the one place someone looks when something is wrong. Fix: ask the question directly, per file. Stricter, not looser — an unapplied file and the MAX_PER_RUN budget case are both still caught; an orphan row is now REPORTED with the exact one-row DELETE that clears it. Witness: `replay-completeness-guard.mjs` in ci.yml, refusing count-equality success AND requiring per-file membership, observed catching the real pre-fix line. Fourth shape of the day's theme: a proxy for truth installed where the truth itself was available. | accepted | A | poetech | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, EXECUTION-OUTCOME-OBSERVABILITY, SOVEREIGN-FIRST, LESSONS-LEARNED, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, SURFACE-PREMISE |

| [DR-0333](DR-0333-the-colg-sermon-intake-way-the-wednesday-email-and-the-youtube-pass.md) | **The COLG sermon-intake Way — every bg@ email becomes a lesson the day it lands, and a second pass when the sermon reaches YouTube.** Darrell, 2026-09-06: sermons from The Church Of The Living God arrive as email and must become lessons the same day, with another pass when the sermon reaches YouTube. Measured against the live mailbox (201 threads) rather than assumed, and four facts are not what an intake would guess: (1) the email BODY IS EMPTY — every word of the sermon is in a `.docx` attachment, so a body-reading intake concludes nothing was sent; (2) the Poe addresses are on **Cc, never To**, so a `to:` filter matches nothing and the trigger must key on the SENDER; (3) the cadence is **Wednesday carrying the preceding Sunday's sermon** (older subjects say so outright: 03-25 → "FROM 03-22-2026 SERMON"), so the Way keys on the email arriving, never a weekday; (4) **BG is the sender, not always the preacher** — the subject names Pastor McCray or Pastor Forman on many weeks, and attribution must follow it or DR-0190 is breached by crediting the wrong servant. The message is the OCCASION, credited and never reproduced; every verse fetched verbatim from the in-repo corpus rather than retyped out of the document or trusted from its cited NKJV/NIV/EEB; private people announced in service never enter a lesson. Pass two is a DIFFERENT source (the preaching, not the points) and may never be skipped because pass one exists — but it depends on the NAS trickle and a fresh `choir_sermons` sync, and this sandbox cannot reach YouTube at all. Standing instance of the typographic-theology conflict clause: the 08-19 subject capitalises the adversary's name and that is not copied through. | accepted | n/a | colg, poetech | WORD-FIRST, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, TRUST-BUT-VERIFY, SPOKEN-TEACHINGS-ARE-BUILD-INPUT, STANDING-CONSENT, REVIEW-OUR-WAYS, EXCELLENCE-STANDARD, DECISION-RECORDS |

| [DR-0334](DR-0334-web-push-and-an-honest-live-signal.md) | **Web Push, and a live signal we can stand behind — the phone was never notified because nothing was ever built.** Darrell, 2026-09-06: "My phone didn't notify me of the livestream... why not." Traced first: `sw.js` had FOUR listeners and NO `push` handler, no `pushManager.subscribe`, no VAPID, no `push_subscriptions` table across 150+ migrations, and no sender anywhere — every notification the app could raise was a FOREGROUND `new Notification()` needing the tab open. The repo had already said so about itself (2026-07-27 review, DR-0231 §P3) and it stayed staged until someone missed a service. A second, quieter gap: nothing to push ON — `church-live.js` computes "live" from a hardcoded weekly window and never asks whether a stream started. Built: RFC 8291/8292 on `crypto.subtle` alone (no `web-push` dep — Node-only, will not run in the Pages Function), an SW push + notificationclick + pushsubscriptionchange path, per-topic opt-in subscriptions, and a sender whose authorization is PROVEN BY THE DATABASE (the `live` send writes `church_live_state` with the CALLER'S OWN JWT; RLS decides, so no drifting function-side copy of the roster rule). The live signal is DECLARED, never inferred — a schedule window stays a UI hint and can never trigger a push, because announcing a service that never started is a fabricated state delivered into a pocket. A message push names the SENDER and never the message: DMs are E2E encrypted and a lock screen is public. Brakes fitted to an event-driven sender: a 2000-device budget that REPORTS the cap, a UNIQUE `dedupe_key` lock that `fanOut` refuses to run without, and an unsubscribe that tears down the browser subscription even when the DB delete fails. Its own gates caught three real defects, including a phishing primitive in the first draft (`//evil.example` passed a leading-slash check) and a replay leg that would have silently reverted the overlay redefinition. HONEST LIMIT: interop is UNVERIFIED — the RFC host is egress-blocked, so no published test vector; a round trip proves self-consistency, not conformance, and the claim closes only when a real phone buzzes. | accepted | C | colg, poetech | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, DATA-AS-EMPOWERMENT, EXECUTION-OUTCOME-OBSERVABILITY, COMMUNITY-FIRST-MISSION, LEGAL-PRIVACY-BOUNDARY, SOVEREIGN-FIRST, LESSONS-LEARNED |

| [DR-0335](DR-0335-the-descent-taught-both-fences-power-destroyed-person-not.md) | **The descent is taught with BOTH fences — his power was destroyed at the cross, his person was not, and the hours Scripture does not narrate stay unwritten.** Darrell, 2026-09-07: "Did Jesus destroy the devil when He went to hell for 3 days and nights according to the biblical scriptures?" The subject is one of the few where BOTH errors are common, and where DR-0098 / DR-0100 / DR-0076 collide unless the position is written once: a popular teaching supplies a three-day battle Scripture never narrates (fabrication), and the reflex correction retreats into "we can't know" (false skepticism about something the Word states plainly). Decided, with both fences up: (1) the disarming is REAL and DATED AT THE CROSS — "It is finished" was said BEFORE He died, Colossians 2:14-15 puts the public triumph "in it", Hebrews 2:14 names the weapon as the dying itself; (2) the adversary was NOT annihilated, proved from letters written AFTER the resurrection that still describe him walking (1 Peter 5:8; Ephesians 6:11-12; Revelation 12:12), with `katargeō` (G2673) = rendered inoperative and 1 John 3:8 naming the WORKS as the object; (3) the three dates stay apart — verdict (John 12:31), binding (Revelation 20:2-3), fire (Revelation 20:10) — which dissolves the apparent contradiction without an argument; (4) the hadēs (G86) / geenna (G1067) distinction is TAUGHT from the Word, since Revelation 20:14 casts one into the other; (5) the un-narrated hours stay unwritten and the silence is said out loud (Deuteronomy 29:29), because every addition of that kind moves the adversary toward the center of a book about the Son; (6) proportion is doctrinal here — four Gospels on the Lamb, one verse on his end — the teaching-side sibling of DR-0099. The popular teaching may be NAMED to educate past it by the Word, never staged as a co-equal view, and no teacher is named or weighed (the DR-0098 line). Worked example ships as L130 (weeks 128 → 129) with a 23-assertion gate that enforces the position rather than trusting the prose, including a negative pattern that fails on any invented fight narrative added later. Verbatim discipline held at **173 quoted spans** with an intentionally EMPTY NOT_SCRIPTURE allowlist. Two real defects caught before shipping and both now proven-to-catch: a quotation **welded Colossians 2:14 and 2:15 across the verse boundary** (both halves genuine, the concatenation not), and the **child level under-quoted Revelation 1:18** by dropping "of hell and" — the DR-0100 failure at the youngest band. | [DR-0336](DR-0336-the-vapid-keys-install-themselves-and-the-public-half-has-one-source.md) | **The VAPID keys install themselves, and the public half of the pair has exactly one source.** Darrell, 2026-09-08, one word: "Connectbot?" DR-0334 shipped the whole push stack and then recorded the keys in the Cloudflare environment as HIS to set; the one-word question was him looking for the road, holding the phone SSH client he uses for the NAS. Literal answer no — ConnectBot reaches the NAS, the keys live on the Pages project. Useful answer: the premise under the question was wrong (DR-0108 — a stated must-be-by-hand is a premise to challenge), because the repo ALREADY HOLDS `CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN` + `CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID` — the deploy uses both on every merge. So `push-vapid-keys.yml` mints the pair with OUR OWN `generateVapidKeys` (Web Crypto P-256, no npm dep) and installs it via `wrangler pages secret bulk` inside one job: the private half is generated `0600`, uploaded, shredded, and NEVER printed — the same DR-0249 line, "credentials only he holds" is smaller than "his hands must type it." The brake here is not the timer class: a ROTATION permanently rejects every existing subscription (a push service binds each one to the key it was created with, and no device is told — people just stop being notified), so an already-configured project is a no-op that changes nothing and an overwrite requires typing ROTATE-AND-INVALIDATE-EVERY-DEVICE into the dispatch form. Second, larger fix: the public half is now served from THE SAME ENVIRONMENT THAT SIGNS (`/api/push-key`), because the original wiring set the two halves in two places at two different times — `VITE_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY` inlined at build, `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY` at runtime — with nothing checking they matched. Drifted, the control says ON truthfully, the sender reports success truthfully, the push service answers 403 to every request, and NO PHONE EVER BUZZES with nothing able to say why — the exact 2026-09-06 gap, one layer up. Held by a proven-to-catch test: an explicit `configured:false` from the server does NOT fall back to the build-time key (the server is authoritative about its own environment), while an UNREACHABLE endpoint does (different fact). Shape-checked before serving, so a mis-set key is reported not-configured instead of throwing inside `subscribe()` on someone's phone. Verified, not claimed: the job reads the secret names back from Cloudflare and then asks the LIVE SITE whether it is serving a key (DR-0125). HONEST GAP UNCHANGED: interop is still UNVERIFIED until a real phone receives a real notification — installing the keys makes the first attempt possible, not proven. | accepted | A | poetech, colg | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, EXECUTION-OUTCOME-OBSERVABILITY, SOVEREIGN-FIRST, COMMUNITY-FIRST-MISSION, DRIVE-DONT-DELEGATE, REVIEW-OUR-WAYS |
| [DR-0338](DR-0338-a-brake-may-bound-the-work-it-may-never-bias-the-finding.md) | **A brake may bound the work; it may never bias the finding.** Not a directive — the loop's own DR-0103 §4 (between prompts, pull the next item forward) sent this session to `scripts/review-watcher.mjs` to pick the next overdue item after L134 merged and deployed, and **the instrument could not be read**: five consecutive byte-identical rows (`INDEX.md · overdue 15d · due 2026-08-25`) that are five DIFFERENT promises sharing a file and a date. Three decisions. (1) **A report row names the COMMITMENT, not only its record** — `re-reviews.js` already computes a per-clause `detail` for exactly this, with a comment dated 2026-07-30 recording the same defect, and the in-app surface renders it while `formatWatchReport` (the path feeding the daily drive, the CI job summary and the rolling issue) dropped it: solved once in the engine, silently reintroduced in the layer most people read. (2) **A brake bounds the WORK, never the FINDING** — the ceiling honestly announced `99 item(s) not scanned`, which is P10 behaving, but the run spent its 500 units in EXTRACTION order and sorted by urgency only over the survivors, so which items were withheld depended on where they fell in the scan and a badly overdue one could be dropped while a December one was kept, report still clean; now ordered by urgency BEFORE the ceiling is spent, ceiling untouched (same units, same truncation note, same unbounded coverage). Raising the ceiling is EXPLICITLY REJECTED — silencing an honest signal by weakening the brake DR-0225/P10 require is the failure, not the remedy. (3) **A brake's threshold is BEHAVIOUR, not a comment** — proving (1) and (2), mutating the default `maxItems` 500 → 100000 broke NO test; the ceiling lived only as a default argument and prose, so anyone widening it shipped green. Now pinned behaviourally (501 commitments, no limits, must scan exactly 500 and report 1 unscanned). Three mutations proven-to-catch, the third only after its gate existed — that survival is why it is a decision and not a note. MEASURED on `bc0435d`, and it is not a happy number: **599 dated commitments in the repo, 500 of them OVERDUE** — the whole budget is consumed by past-due items, so the 99 withheld are past due too. NOT DECIDED HERE and named as the Governor's call: closing that backlog is untouched work materially larger than this change (oldest-first sweep, triage by tier, or bulk-closing commitments whose conditions were already met). (4) **A reader must see the ledger's own closures** — sweeping the oldest cluster found five records carrying the same 2026-07-12 commitment for the incident #722 serve-layer decision **made 59 days ago**, with LESSONS-LEARNED's own `**CLOSED 2026-07-10 by DR-0155:**` sitting exactly where the extractor looks and invisible to it, because `DONE_MARKER_RE`'s prefix admitted only whitespace, an em-dash, a colon and an opening bracket while the ledger closes in real markdown (backtick, period, bold asterisks, closing paren); the punctuation class now tracks how the ledger writes, the marker stays ALL-CAPS and case-sensitive since the capitals are what stop prose closing a commitment. **THE HYPOTHESIS THIS KILLED, recorded because the number matters more than the story:** missed closures were expected to explain the 500 and DO NOT — 609 scanned, 6 closures already seen, exactly **2** missed; my first probe said three because I wrote it case-insensitively and it matched the prose "closed the same day", the very false-close the ALL-CAPS rule prevents, committed by my own measuring tool. Corrected position: **the 500 overdue are genuinely overdue as far as the ledger records** — under-counted by 2, not 400 — so the backlog is real work rather than a reporting artifact, a bigger finding than the one I set out to confirm. Two more mutations proven-to-catch (narrow prefix restored; marker case-insensitive). (5) **Narrate a commitment date without the ledger's own token** — closing the #722 cluster (four records marked DONE on VERIFIED evidence that DR-0155 shipped all three parts: guard file live, its 16 proven-to-catch assertions green, propagation gate present) moved the count barely at all, because this session's own writing had minted FIVE new commitments: the session note, REV-0252, the DR and this row each quoted the literal token while DESCRIBING the finding, and the extractor read all five narrations as live promises — it cannot know intent and should not try. A record that describes a commitment writes the bare date; the token is reserved for an actual promise. DELIBERATELY NOT GATED with the why (DR-0075): separating a promise from a description of one misfires on legitimate prose, and a guard that fires where it should not is noise, which is how a guard gets deleted (REV-0249's lesson); the protection is that the watcher's TOTAL is now watched, so an authoring pass that inflates it surfaces in the next run — re-review: 2026-11-08, and a recurrence is itself the evidence the false-positive cost is worth paying. **NET: 597 commitments after the sweep, down from 599 at session start despite four legitimate new ones this work added** — four real closures (open 59 days, already finished), five phantoms removed. re-review: 2026-10-08 — has the count moved off 500, and did the legible report get used; if not, the instrument is fine and the DISCIPLINE is the finding. | accepted | A | poetech | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, REVIEW-OUR-WAYS, EXECUTION-OUTCOME-OBSERVABILITY, DECISION-RECORDS |

| [DR-0337](DR-0337-the-porter-was-already-in-the-text-a-labor-day-lesson-and-the-two-track-honesty-rule.md) | **The porter was already in the text — a Labor Day lesson, and the two-track honesty rule for history beside Scripture.** Darrell, 2026-09-08: "lesson." plus a telling about the Pullman porters — men hired out of slavery to staff America's sleeping cars and addressed by passengers as George, the owner's first name, because "you didn't have to learn who they were." The occasion is history; the LESSON is Scripture, and the join was not manufactured: **the KJV word for the man at the door of the sheepfold is PORTER**, in a sentence about the exact opposite of what a railway carriage did — "To him the porter openeth... and he calleth his own sheep by name" (John 10:3). In the house of Yahweh the porters are an ORDAINED Levitical office, NAMED in the permanent record (Shallum, Akkub, Talmon, Ahiman — 1 Chronicles 9:17), 212 of them ordained by David and Samuel (9:22), trusted with the treasury (9:26), given authority and the watch (Mark 13:34); a reigning king wanted the job (Psalms 84:10). The word a nation used to make men interchangeable is the word Yahweh uses for a named servant at His own gate. From there, the Word on its own terms: Yahweh names and is NEVER ONCE recorded un-naming (Genesis 17:5; 32:28; John 1:42; Isaiah 56:5; Revelation 2:17); the first person recorded naming HIM is a discarded slave woman, and the name is about being SEEN (Genesis 16:13); He positions the overlooked on purpose (Genesis 39:2; 2 Kings 5:2-3; 1 Corinthians 1:27-28); the stone the BUILDERS refuse is the one He sets in the corner (Psalms 118:22); and He legislated the labourer's wage — same day, before sundown, "for he is poor, and setteth his heart upon it" (Deuteronomy 24:15) — millennia before any nation had a labour movement, which makes it the real Labor Day text. THE TWO-TRACK HONESTY RULE, decided and now gated: Scripture is fetched verbatim and checkable (198 spans, empty NOT_SCRIPTURE allowlist — our emphasis wears capitals, never quotes); documented history is stated PLAINLY without hedging (DR-0100 — under-claiming a truth is its own lie); commonly-cited ranges are LABELLED as ranges; a compression in the telling is corrected gently in our prose with its reason (the Brotherhood was the first Black-led union chartered by the AFL and first to win a contract with a major corporation — accuracy makes the story harder to dismiss, not smaller); and provenance is named, since the Scripture was verified against the corpus while the history is well-established knowledge, not fetched. BOTH FENCES gated, because this material wounds when preached bare: under-claiming is forbidden (Exodus 21:16 sentences the man-stealer to death; 1 Timothy 1:10 lists menstealers as contrary to sound doctrine), over-claiming equally so (Genesis 50:20 holds they thought evil AND Yahweh meant it unto good as TWO facts — collapsing them turns a comfort into a defence of the men who did it), the EDIT IS THE TELL (Bibles were printed with portions cut out; had the text supported the system nobody would have needed a blade — Deuteronomy 4:2, Acts 20:27, and the restored suppressed half at Colossians 4:1), and no partisan contest is staged (DR-0098). Ends on the Son who was looked away from (Isaiah 53:3), took the servant's form (Philippians 2:7), and was given the Name above every name (Philippians 2:9) — a worship lesson, not a grievance lesson, asserted by the gate. Two real defects caught and both now proven-to-catch: a SHORTENED quotation (2 Chronicles 8:14 trimmed to "for so had God commanded" — three words short of the verse and not in the corpus at all), and SIX generic "God" in our own authored voice; the DR-0210 check strips quoted spans first, because a check that did not would demand we corrupt the text — and it now carries its COMPLEMENT, that the KJV's own "God" must still be PRESENT inside quotations, closing the loophole where a blind find-replace would have passed. Register measured, not claimed: child 1.30 / teen 6.70 / adult 8.00 / senior 12.30, not inverted, child under the ceiling, and the shrink-only baseline gained NO new offenders. TWO numbering collisions in one afternoon: authored as L131, renumbered to L134 when a fetch of main showed L131-L133 merged mid-authoring, shipped as L135 after main merged its OWN L134 (#1482) while this PR's CI ran. AND A CORRECTION TO THIS RECORD'S OWN FIRST DRAFT, kept rather than edited away: it said the collision was caught "not by a gate," which was false — `living-lessons-id-collision.test.js` has existed since 2026-09-02, built after two sessions independently claimed L114, and asserts exactly this. The SECOND collision locates the real catch: it surfaced as a git add/add conflict on the test FILENAME, which is the very mechanism that gate's own header calls "luck, not a check" — had the filenames differed, git would have auto-merged both modules in silence. That silent case is what the gate is for, and merging main into a PR head is the first moment both claims exist in one tree, so it fires ON THE PR, in CI, before main is touched. MEASURED, not asserted: the duplicate was reconstructed and the suite went red naming both ids. Decision: no new gate and no re-review date — the check exists, is sufficient, and fires at the only moment it can; fetching main first is a courtesy that saves a merge cycle, never the safety. Claiming a gap without grepping for the gate that closes it is the DR-0107 cited-but-unread failure in miniature. | accepted | n/a | poetech, colg | WORD-FIRST, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, TEACH-DONT-DEBATE, SPEAK-ESTABLISHED-FACT, SPOKEN-TEACHINGS-ARE-BUILD-INPUT, COMMUNITY-FIRST-MISSION, EXCELLENCE-STANDARD, DECISION-RECORDS |

Proven live in a rolled-back transaction before pushing, rollback verified; the migration-replay-order guard caught a real revert of `obligation_record` and named the fix. THE BRIGHT LINE, asserted in the smoke: after sorting, the same child reads ZERO obligations, ZERO obligation_lines and ZERO transactions. | [DR-0361](DR-0361-a-provider-switched-off-is-never-offered-and-the-cutover-dropped-google.md) | **A provider switched off is never offered, and the cutover dropped Google.** Darrell 2026-09-11, 11:58am, mid-meeting at the Love Corner with the finance steward: *"Google is getting an error message when logging in... help."* A member tapped Continue with Google and got GoTrue's raw 400 JSON -- `Unsupported provider: provider is not enabled` -- in a popup with no way back, and the dialog itself showed nothing. Traced to the literal config: the sovereign stack's `auth` service carried NO `GOTRUE_EXTERNAL_*` key of any kind, because on hosted Supabase Google was enabled in the DASHBOARD and a dashboard setting is not a file, so it never travelled with DR-0307's cutover; and `signInWithOAuth` builds the authorize URL client-side so it can never error, leaving the app blind. A second, hidden break found in the same trace: `ADDITIONAL_REDIRECT_URLS` named only the root and `/poetech-app/`, so a return to `/love-corner` would have been refused and bounced to `SITE_URL`, silently dropping the popup handshake marker. Decisions: the provider lives in `docker-compose.yml`, credential-gated like SMTP so an unset secret keeps the stack healthy; the callback is stated literally as the same-origin `/sb` door, never derived from the still-loopback `API_EXTERNAL_URL`; a new append-only `add_csv_kv` converges the allow-list to `https://poetech.us/**` without rewriting an operator's entries; `lib/auth-providers.js` reads GoTrue's own `external` provider map and gates both Google entry points, PRIMED ON MOUNT and read SYNCHRONOUSLY so the click keeps the user gesture `window.open` needs; "could not ask" is the one deliberate inversion of unknown-is-never-good (DR-0125) and proceeds, because taking away a working login path is worse than the JSON popup; the blocked message names Google and names email, and never shows a raw error code. The lawful human tail is exactly a secret value onto a device plus one Google console click, both in `infra/nas-supabase/GOOGLE-SIGN-IN.md` with the DR-0108 challenge run. Proof: `oauth-provider-preflight-guard.test.js` (27) with four proven-to-catch cases, `auth-modal-render.test.jsx` (+3) on the real mounted dialog, verified to fail with the guard neutered; `add_csv_kv` exercised against four env-file states | accepted | B | platform | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, APP-IS-PRIMARY, EXCELLENCE-STANDARD, DETERMINISTIC-FIRST, DECISION-RECORDS |
| [DR-0362](DR-0362-one-cash-app-statement-becomes-every-givers-record-and-the-deposit-proves-itself.md) | **One Cash App statement becomes every giver's record, and the deposit proves itself.** The Love Corner finance steward and church office, 2026-09-11, finding the requirement out loud: *"if you got person a, b, c, and let's just say they totaled a thousand dollars... you could just transfer the whole thousand dollars, and then on your statement, it's gonna tell you every person and what they gave"* -- against today's cost, *"the transfer, put it in Excel, then put it in my other report, then create the reports for the end of the month"* -- and the stake that governs every call: *"we get thousands of dollars... so applying it to the RIGHT person."* Traced first: the office-side shape (`service_offerings`, `giving_reconciliations`, `donor_giving`, including the value `claim_kind='reconciled-from-online'`) was fully modelled in `schema-v2.7-church.sql` and had ZERO application code; no importer anywhere could turn one row into many; `bank-formats.js` had no counterparty role, the one column a bank statement lacks; and giving had no month. Decisions: `lib/cashapp-statement.js` reads by column NAME never position, proving accepted+rejected===source on every read, with a top-up, a send, a refund, a pending payment and a non-dollar row each kept out of giving and still counted; `lib/giving-batch.js` is the split -- one payout plus its gifts becomes one batch and N claims, gifts ride the first transfer at or after them, an undeposited tail is visible not absent, and reconciliation names the exact difference with NO tolerance band (one cent of drift is drift) and adjusts nothing; `lib/giving-donor-match.js` proposes who a name is and never decides, naming its basis, reporting a tie AS a tie, and remembering confirmations so the queue shrinks every month; `lib/giving-reports.js` gives the month-end, by-giver and per-person records where an unidentified giver keeps his name, his money and a mark, a high-confidence proposal is still rendered as the RAW name (a proposal shown as a member is how a wrong attribution goes invisible), and every report states how much of itself is unconfirmed. `giving_records` is deliberately untouched -- it is the giver's own private ledger (0184). Stated honestly: the exact Cash App header spelling is documented-not-measured, one real export closes it (re-review 2026-09-18). A silent bug found on the way and fixed: `DebtStatementUpload` read `plan.toAdd` while `planAccountImport` returns `{txns, duplicates:Number}`, so a debt WITH an account imported nothing while displaying "0 new" -- gated now by a contract test reading both real values (P52). And the same shape one level up: `npm run verify` -- the command this repo documents as "the real gate" -- was lint+vitest while CI runs 13 guard SCRIPTS as separate steps, so a local green of 13,388 tests did not predict a red CI; `verify` now chains all 13 in CI's order and `verify-covers-ci-guards.test.js` fails the build if CI gains a step verify cannot reach. NOT done: no surface, no sync, no bank-row match yet (re-review 2026-09-18). | [DR-0363](DR-0363-the-sum-rule-refuses-inside-the-function-not-at-commit.md) | **The sum rule refuses inside the function, not at commit.** DR-0359 enforced "line items must add up to the payment" with a DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED constraint trigger — deferred on purpose, because a four-line breakdown is written a row at a time. But deferred means it fires at COMMIT, not when `obligation_set_lines` returns, so inside any open transaction the function wrote a breakdown that did not add up and reported success. The rls-isolation leg caught it on merge (runs 149–152, red on main): "FAIL: a breakdown SHORT of the payment was accepted". The smoke was right; the migration was wrong. WHY THE LOCAL PROBE MISSED IT, and the lesson kept: the pre-push probe had written `SET CONSTRAINTS ALL IMMEDIATE` to observe the trigger — exactly the condition the real function never creates. Arranging the world so a check fires is not proof that it fires; DR-0076 §3 is sharpened to proven-to-catch IN THE SHAPE THE CODE ACTUALLY RUNS IN. Production was less wrong (PostgREST runs each RPC in its own transaction, so the trigger did fire at commit) but the function's own return value was a lie. Decisions: migration 0205 — `obligation_set_lines` checks the sum itself, after the inserts and before the return; the deferred trigger STAYS as the backstop for any other path; the smoke is unchanged because it was correct. Proof: the failure reproduced then fixed against the live schema in one rolled-back transaction without forcing constraint mode (short accepted=true before, false after; exact accepted; 3 lines intact); `door-economics.test.js` (33). (Minted as DR-0361 on a concurrent branch and renumbered on merge per DR-0052; #1527 merged first and keeps 0361 and 0362.) | accepted | B | money | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, DETERMINISTIC-FIRST, DECISION-RECORDS |

WITH ORDINALITY fixed it; after the fix 59 proposed → 59 created → $11,360.19, and a second run skipped all 59. Independent cross-check: the plan's own stated datedBillsTotal is $11,360.19, the sum of all 61 entries is $11,360.19, the import total is $11,360.19 — to the cent. All runs rolled back; production still holds 0 obligations. The smoke carried its own defect too (inserted `family_plans(created_by)`, a column that does not exist), caught before pushing — DR-0361's lesson applied one migration later. | [DR-0367](DR-0367-ask-for-a-tab-and-the-office-decides-and-the-approval-actually-grants.md) | **Ask for a tab, and the office decides — and the approval actually grants.** Darrell 2026-09-11: "have an request and approval process for changing or giving access to the tabs that are staff and work related... based on BG" (BG = Bishop Gwin, already the whole CHURCH_STAFF_EMAILS allowlist). TWO PARTS. (1) `surfaces.js` carried `gate:` on 29 of 56 surfaces as PROSE read by NOTHING; every gated surface now also carries machine-readable `requires` + `whenDenied`, decided by lib/surface-access.js. `hide` where the surface's EXISTENCE is not the viewer's business (Admin, CRM, Forecast, Study, the Observation board); `lock` where a person could legitimately want and ask (Devices, Infra Plan, Video Wall, Harvest, Projects) — because a hidden tab teaches nothing and somebody who SHOULD have access cannot tell "not for me" from "broken". Five hand-written white boxes saying "Sign in with a church staff account" — wrong for anybody already signed in — are replaced by a black LockedSurface tile that says WHAT it is, WHO holds it (never "your administrator"), WHY, and how to ask. (2) THE PREMISE CONFLICT: the staff tabs were gated by an EMAIL ALLOWLIST, so approving could not have granted anything without a code edit and a deploy — an approval queue that cannot grant is theatre. 0211 adds ONE key (`see:church-staff`) to 0126's CLOSED capability allowlist and the shell honours it beside the email list. ONE key not one per tab (every staff surface shares the predicate; a per-tab key would be a promise the app cannot keep) — said out loud on both surfaces. A SEE key, not a write key: 0211 redefines neither capability_area, nor the viewer overlay, nor never_unlockable_tables. DECIDING IS GRANTING — the grant runs through 0126's guarded door BEFORE the row is marked, so an impossible grant raises and writes nothing; there is deliberately NO UPDATE policy. One open ask per person per surface; a decided row must carry who decided it and when (table constraint); nobody decides their own. Two things beside the key changed in set_member_capability and are named rather than left in a diff: an audit_log row (0126 wrote none) and REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon. CAUGHT BY MACHINERY: migration-replay-order-guard refused the first draft — the viewer-readonly leg replays 0126 and would have restored the closed allowlist, leaving approvals raising "unknown capability" in production while every test passed. NOT built: no per-tab keys; no notification to BG (re-review 2026-10-12); no expiry on a grant (re-review 2026-12-12). | accepted | B | platform | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, REALITY-TRACE, SURFACE-PREMISE-CONFLICTS, DETERMINISTIC-FIRST, SAY-WHAT-IS-NOT-BUILT, DECISION-RECORDS |

Five isolation legs replay 0111/0143/0144 against the LIVE database and none replayed 0210. `migration-replay-order-guard` ran green because its pattern required `CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION`, and a RETURN-TYPE change cannot use it — Postgres refuses — so it is forced into DROP+CREATE: the one shape a replacement must take was the one shape the guard could not see (P55). The trigger was never a manual dispatch either -- db-migrate's own final step dispatches the matrix, so every migration merge re-triggered it. The fix IS proven: 0213 applied through the lane, the widened witness passed, 0213's smoke printed PASS, and the four legs that had been reverting it left the function at 13 columns. Sovereign state still unverified (re-review 2026-09-13). | accepted | B | platform | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, REALITY-TRACE, SURFACE-PREMISE-CONFLICTS, DETERMINISTIC-FIRST, SAY-WHAT-IS-NOT-BUILT, DECISION-RECORDS |

Decisions: L142 teaches the METHOD before any objection, with an exhibit provable from the corpus rather than asserted -- Matthew 4:6 quotes Psalm 91 accurately and drops `to keep thee in all thy ways`, and the test asserts that omission against the KJV files; 2 Peter 3:16 keeps BOTH of Darrell's causes (`unlearned and unstable` who `wrest`) and puts the destruction on the wrester; every true half is conceded first and fully, because most people never trimmed anything but were handed it short; the historical record is stated with no hedge (Augusta 1845 and the SBC's own 1995 repentance, the 1838 sale of 272 people, St. George's 1787, the 1915 Klan under a former preacher, and the 1807 Bible printed with the deliverance and all the Psalms cut out) and then the Word OUT-CHARGES the objection (man-stealing a capital crime, `menstealers`, `I never knew you`, and Yahweh `against the prophets... that use their tongues, and say, He saith`); the oldest instance answers the premise -- religious leadership killed the Son using the government (`We have no king but Caesar`) and He predicted the pattern outright; paper and perception are refused as proof because `man looketh on the outward appearance`; and it ends on an invitation rather than a proof, `O taste and see that the LORD is good`. Proof: 153 quoted spans ALL verbatim KJV with the gate catching three real transcription errors mid-authoring; every historical claim fetched and cited, not recalled; reading level measured child 0.66 / teen 8.15 / senior 15.58 with no new baseline offenders; Yahweh 58x in our voice, zero generic, zero capitalized adversary. | [DR-0379](DR-0379-the-family-rent-roll-syncs-before-the-ledger-verify-and-an-unlinked-edit-self-heals.md) | **The family rent roll syncs before the ledger verify, and an unlinked edit self-heals.** Darrell 2026-09-13, from Christina's device: *"Christina has updated information about tenants and I don't see the updates... obviously this system is to sync or family!!!!! fix it!"* and, approving the approach, *"yes, loosen the gate and have her re-sync."* MEASURED: the cloud `rentals` table was frozen since 2026-08-07 — 1508 Williamsburg still `$0`/"1508 HH" while her screen showed `$1,150`, `$1,000` on 709 Commercial, and the "Holly Hill" rename; not one edit had reached the cloud (verified row-by-row against the live DB). ROOT CAUSE in `poe-financial-mvp-v28.jsx`: the whole rentals sync sits behind `numericSyncVerifiedAt` (set only by the "Verify Balances" wizard) — a member with real numeric data who never completed it gets the wizard and the effect returns before any rentals sync runs, and the per-CRUD guards require the flag too; and `updateRental` additionally required a pre-existing `remoteUuid` and SILENTLY skipped otherwise (a DR-0076 §8 silent failure). That wizard protects the financial LEDGER, and never should have gated the property list. DECISIONS: (1) the three rental CRUD paths gate on `authSession && !isAnyDemoMode && !reviewerMode`, decoupled from the verify flag — the ledger keeps it, and `reviewerMode` added so the reviewer lens (DR-0241) writes nothing; (2) an unlinked edit self-heals — `syncRentalEdit` in `lib/rental-write.js` (not the budget-frozen monolith, DR-0078) routes a linked row to `updateRow` and an unlinked one to a new `upsert` that resolves by the live `(instance_id, slug)` UNIQUE index and UPDATEs or INSERTs, resolve-then-write because that index is PARTIAL (`WHERE slug IS NOT NULL`) and `ON CONFLICT` cannot target it; (3) recovery is by re-sync, not by hand — no rent figures written to production by the agent (DR-0076 §9). Proven-to-catch: `table-sync-upsert.test.js` (existing row UPDATEs, never a duplicate) and `rental-write-selfheal.test.js` (linked vs unlinked routing). verify green 950 files / 14,171 tests, lint clean, monolith freeze held. RESIDUAL (DR-0075): closes the UPLOAD direction; the download-side subscribe stays gated until a follow-up that won't let the pull-merge clobber an un-pushed local edit — re-review 2026-09-27.
