| [DR-0378](DR-0378-a-fault-is-addressed-to-the-office-never-broadcast.md) | **A fault is addressed to the office, never broadcast.** **THIS RECORD EXISTS BECAUSE I BROKE DR-0111 AND THE ARI-GUARD CAUGHT IT BEFORE DARRELL SAW IT.** Having just shipped DR-0377, I told him that pushing a fault to the office was *"the timer-driven class and is Darrell's call, not something to slip in"* -- and stopped. That is exactly what DR-0225 names: *"Citing this section to defer building, or re-presenting decided work as an open question, is a DR-0111 violation."* The three-brakes rule governs what ships ACTIVE; it is a BUILD requirement, never a stall, and DR-0247 is blunter -- *"parking agreed work on a human start is the DR-0111-class violation."* I used a safety rule as a reason to stop working, which is the opposite of what it says. THE DECISION THAT ACTUALLY NEEDED MAKING, AND I MADE IT: a fault push is NOT shaped like `live`, and getting that wrong would have been a real leak. `live` fans out to every opted-in device in a tenant -- correct for a congregation, wrong for "this business's shop is broken," where the entitled audience is the OFFICE (owner/admin, the same predicate 0216's RLS uses). The tempting shortcut was to inherit the instance broadcast and rely on "only the steward screen offers the toggle" -- **a UI gate pretending to be an access gate**, which would tell any customer who reached the topic by another route when Shay's shop is failing. So an explicit office audience is REQUIRED, routing `fault` through `audienceQuery`'s per-person path rather than the per-instance one: **there is no code path that broadcasts a fault**, and the validator refuses the attempt rather than quietly fanning out. Decisions: `fault` joins the CLOSED enumerated `SENDABLE_TOPICS`, whose pinning test exists precisely so a broadcast channel cannot be added unseen -- updated deliberately rather than deleted; the audience is mandatory and explicit, with the reason in the error; **the fault id IS the dedupe key and that is load-bearing**, since 0217 FOLDS a repeat into the same row and returns the SAME id, so a door failing four hundred times buzzes the office EXACTLY ONCE and only a genuinely new open fault earns a second buzz -- the storm was already one row, this makes it one notification; NO error text and NO customer text on a lock screen, the same posture `messageAnnouncement` takes for DM bodies since a push renders in public to whoever holds the phone (asserted against `unknown pipeline`, `crm_capture_lead`, `SELECT` and `@`) -- the push says THAT something is wrong and HOW WIDELY, the app says what; and the count is carried because it IS the severity, "hit it 47 times" being the difference between a snag and a shop closed all morning. BRAKES DESIGNED IN RATHER THAN DEFERRED (DR-0248 budget + lock): budget is the title/body caps, MAX_DEVICES_PER_SEND, and an audience of a named handful rather than a tenant; the lock is the dedupe key, enforced by the key rather than by a caller remembering. NOT DONE, and the reason is VERIFIED SECURITY rather than scheduling: the trigger is server-side and deliberately NOT built here, because the obvious shortcut -- the client calling push-send after filing its fault -- would let ANY ANONYMOUS VISITOR BUZZ SHAY'S PHONE ON DEMAND, a far worse abuse vector than the spoofable row 0217 already accepts (a row is read when she opens a board; a push is an intrusion into her pocket), so the client deliberately does not call it and the trigger belongs on a server path that reads open faults and resolves the office itself -- re-review 2026-09-20; no office member has subscribed to the topic because nothing offers the toggle yet; and no live fault has been pushed end to end. Proof: verify green 953 files / 14,252 tests; 11 new tests; proven-to-catch by removing the mandatory-audience check -- the exact mistake that would have turned a private fault into a tenant broadcast | accepted | B | business | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, DO-THE-WORK, APP-IS-PRIMARY, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, DECISION-RECORDS |
| [DR-0377](DR-0377-a-door-that-breaks-reports-itself.md) | **A door that breaks reports itself.** The half of DR-0374 that DR-0376 deliberately left open, pulled forward from its 2026-09-27 date because it is buildable now (DR-0236). DR-0376 gave a customer a way to SAY "this is broken"; it did nothing about the real finding, that NOTHING TELLS ANYONE ON ITS OWN. Sterling's order was refused on every attempt for the whole life of the door and the client's entire response was `console.warn('[crm-sync] capture failed:', error)` (crm-sync.js:140) -- printed on his own phone, seen by nobody, kept by nothing. A form is a real improvement but still WAITS on a customer choosing to speak up, and most people who hit a dead form just leave: a system that can only find out when somebody is kind is not instrumented, it is LUCKY, and Sterling was the kindness. Decisions: the door files its own SYSTEM-sourced row into the same book the office already reads (`door_fault_report`, 0217) so there is no second inbox and no new screen to remember; DEDUPED, because a broken door is broken for everyone and a hundred visitors must not become a hundred rows burying the customer voice -- an identical OPEN fault inside 24h increments `occurrences` and moves `last_seen_at`, and **the count IS the alert** ("47 times since 9am" says what one row never could); a fold NEVER reopens what the office handled, since a closed fault breaking again is genuinely new and earns its own row rather than a silent bump on settled work; A SEPARATE FUNCTION rather than a flag on `door_feedback_submit`, because that customer path was verified on both databases hours earlier (md5 63cf8950...) and is not being touched, re-tested or put at risk to add a second behaviour; the office is told a SENTENCE not a stack trace, with the machine detail capped at 300 chars (a raw error is noise to a steward and an unbounded one on her screen is an injection surface); a machine is NEVER displayed as a person -- system rows are labelled "the app noticed this", carry their count, and say "Written by the app, not a person -- there is nobody to reply to" where a customer row would offer contact details, with open faults getting their own banner because "your door is reporting a problem on its own" is not a queue item; and a fault report NEVER makes things worse, never throwing and never surfacing, with DR-0374's honest error panel still rendering exactly as before -- the self-report is IN ADDITION to telling the customer the truth, never instead of it. EXPLICITLY NOT THE TIMER-DRIVEN CLASS, so the three-brakes rule does not attach (DR-0247/0248): no loop to budget, lock or stop -- a row is written when a real user action really failed and the office PULLS it; a push notification WOULD be that class and is left to its own decision rather than smuggled in. SPOOFING STATED PLAINLY: the seam is anon-callable so a row can claim to be a system fault, the same exposure DR-0376 already accepted, bounded by the same flood brake -- and the alternative, trusting only server-observed faults, CANNOT SEE a failure happening in the customer's browser, which is exactly where Sterling's happened; visible and spoofable beats invisible. Proof: verify green 953 files / 14,241 tests; 17 tests MOUNTING the door and failing a real order with the real refusal shape; proven-to-catch twice, each by one precisely-targeted test -- removing the self-report fails the "calls door_fault_report" test, removing the fold fails the "FOLDS a repeat" test. METHODOLOGY NOTE KEPT because it repeated tonight: my first attempt at the second break DID NOT APPLY, the suite stayed green, and 0 failures meant NOTHING -- an incomplete break rather than a weak test, verified before any conclusion was drawn, the same trap as DR-0373's. **EXECUTED AND WITNESSED ON BOTH DATABASES** (merged `d591bc2b`): `door_fault_report` reads md5 `f8f4382d4f2bf37200d6aa065758b654` on hosted by direct query AND the identical value on the SOVEREIGN database (`sovereign-read` run 34743949911, `---MISSING--- none`, `sovereign_replay=223`); anon can call it, the table stays closed to anon (false/false), the new columns and `source` CHECK are present, and the policy count is STILL 8 after the ALTER -- office plus both overlays intact, which was the specific regression risk worth checking rather than assuming. **DECISION 4 IS NOW MEASURED RATHER THAN ASSERTED:** `door_feedback_submit` reads md5 `63cf89507ec6cc0bf35253e461198c3c` on both databases, byte-for-byte what it was BEFORE 0217 existed, so "a separate function leaves the verified customer path untouched" is a fact rather than a design intention. NOT DONE: only the ORDER form reports itself so far, while the same door's classes, messages and showcase seams still fail quietly -- the pattern is now one function call so widening it is small, but claiming the door is instrumented would overstate it, re-review 2026-09-27; nothing reaches the office while they are AWAY from the board, since this makes a fault findable rather than pushed; and no live fault has been observed end to end, the sandbox having no route to poetech.us -- re-review 2026-09-20 | accepted | B | business | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, APP-IS-PRIMARY, REALITY-TRACE, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, DECISION-RECORDS |
| [DR-0376](DR-0376-every-door-gets-a-way-to-say-this-is-broken.md) | **Every door gets a way to say "this is broken."** DR-0374's own open item, pulled forward rather than left on its date (DR-0236). Darrell asked *"does Moore Divahs have a feedback process?"* while Sterling's broken order was being fixed; the answer was NO, and the consequence was already measured -- his inquiry failed on EVERY attempt for the entire life of her door, `crm_leads` held zero rows, nothing alerted anyone, and the only reason it was ever discovered is that he told Shay and she told Darrell. A favour from a customer is not a process, and `grep -i feedback` over MooreDoor.jsx returned nothing -- for HER door and every other the registry serves. Neither existing channel covers it, checked rather than assumed: `public.feedback` requires sign-in and enrols the writer into `poe-family` (feedback-sync.js:82), reaching Darrell rather than the business owner and sitting behind OUR login; and `send_business_message` opens with `IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'not authenticated'` -- read from the live function body -- which is SOUND, since `customer_user_id` IS the thread identity, so an anonymous message has no thread to belong to. THE PROPERTY THAT DECIDES EVERYTHING: the person who meets a broken door is the LEAST likely to be signed in, and both channels fail that test. Decisions: the seam is anon-callable by design (`door_feedback_submit`, 0216 -- SECURITY DEFINER, granted to anon, recording `auth.uid()` as a NULLABLE author rather than a gate); **NO door or area allowlist in SQL, which is 0215's lesson applied the same day it was learned** -- 0215 existed because a hard-coded pipeline allowlist and a second hand-maintained JS registry disagreed and refused every order for months, so 0216 pins the tenant by a REAL ROW LOOKUP and validates only shape, with the vocabularies living in `door-feedback-sync.js` alone so a new client door (DR-0114) works on the day it ships with nothing to keep in sync, enforced by a test reading the migration with comments stripped; over-long values are TRUNCATED never rejected, because a customer reporting a break must not lose the report to a validation message about a field they did not type; read is office-only with deliberately NO member policy and NO self-read policy, since a customer telling a business something is broken has not published it to other customers, and the table is REVOKE ALL from anon so the anon path is the FUNCTION not the table; a per-instance-per-minute flood brake, generous enough that a real outage still gets through; and THE READ SIDE SHIPS IN THE SAME CHANGE (`DoorReportsSection`, triage order putting what nobody has looked at first and oldest within that) because the form alone would be a write-only hole -- the exact zero-consumers failure DR-0371 named. Proof: verify green 952 files / 14,224 tests; 22 tests MOUNTING the component with no session at all; proven-to-catch twice (gating the form on a session fails 11 of 22; reintroducing 0215's hard-coded area allowlist fails the single-registry test). THREE GATES CAUGHT REAL DEFECTS, each fixed rather than worked around: **the component would have CRASHED on the live door** -- it imported only `useState` while this project uses the CLASSIC JSX runtime, so it compiled, PASSED LINT, and threw `ReferenceError: React is not defined` on first render, found only by mounting (DR-0371's lesson repeating: a test that reads source cannot see a component that cannot render); the legibility guard caught an inline status-pill colour at 3.94:1 and 2.56:1 on the midnight card surface, replaced with themeable classes and the health ledger regenerated WITHOUT touching the shrink-only baseline; and three overlay gates caught 0216 creating an instance-scoped table without re-running the standing overlays, without which a read-only VIEWER could write door_feedback and an ASSISTANT could read what customers told a business privately about its own door (DR-0347 enforced). **EXECUTED AND WITNESSED ON BOTH DATABASES** (merged `5cbde600`): `door_feedback_submit` reads `md5` `63cf89507ec6cc0bf35253e461198c3c` on hosted by direct query AND the same value on the SOVEREIGN database via `sovereign-read` run 34742113110 (`---MISSING--- none`, `sovereign_replay=222`), which proves the definitions byte-identical; anon CAN call the seam and CANNOT read or write the table (`has_table_privilege` false/false), RLS on, and 8 policies present -- `door_feedback_office[ALL]` plus `assistant_scope_*` x4 and `viewer_readonly_*` x3, so the overlays the gates forced in are PROVABLY APPLIED in the database rather than merely written in the file; deploy run 34741979843 on head_sha 5cbde600. Measurement limit stated rather than glossed: the definitions probe reports FUNCTIONS only, so the table's RLS and policies were verified on hosted and are carried on the sovereign side by the replay plus the identical function md5, not read back directly there -- extending the probe to tables and policies is the next turn of DR-0375's instrument. NOT DONE: nobody has filed a report on the live site, jsdom is not a browser and the sandbox has no route to poetech.us -- re-review 2026-09-20; only Moore Divahs has a door today, so client #2's inheritance is asserted from the registry's shape rather than observed; and STILL NOTHING ALERTS ANYONE -- this gives a customer a way to report, it does not page the office when a report lands nor notice a capture RPC failing silently, which is the other half of DR-0374's finding -- re-review 2026-09-27 | accepted | B | business | APP-IS-PRIMARY, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, REALITY-TRACE, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, DECISION-RECORDS |
| [DR-0375](DR-0375-ask-the-live-database-about-any-function-and-reject-the-argument-rather-than-escape-it.md) | **Ask the live database about any function, and reject the argument rather than escape it.** Nobody asked for this; it comes out of DR-0374, where I made a FALSE MEASUREMENT and had to correct a merged decision record -- reading `crm_capture_lead`'s allowlist off the HOSTED project and writing it up as the live state, while `REPOINT-ARMED` means the app runs on the sovereign NAS. Per DR-0108 the WAY is reviewed, not only the bug, and the way was already half-built: `sovereign-read.yml` landed 2026-09-12 for this exact blind spot and did not help me. WHY IT DID NOT: its `definitions` mode carried a HARD-CODED list of seven church/property function names with no argument, so asking a new question meant editing the script, opening a PR and merging it -- which in practice meant NOBODY ASKED, and the default path stayed the hosted mirror that answers instantly and wrongly. An instrument that answers only seven pre-chosen questions is not a general answer to "which database is this claim about," and that is the defect rather than my carelessness alone. Decisions: `functions` becomes an argument (blank keeps the standing seven, so every existing use is unchanged); it is REJECTED, never escaped -- a caller string reaching a SQL `IN` list on a database held by ssh with admin credentials is validated against `^[a-z0-9_]+(,[a-z0-9_]+)*$`, a class that cannot express a quote, space, semicolon or comment marker, with anything else exiting 2 and reading NOTHING rather than partially; validation sits AHEAD of the `NAS_SSH_KEY` check so a rejected argument provably touches no network; `md5(prosrc)` is reported per function, which is the drift check this whole class needed, since an identical md5 on hosted and sovereign PROVES the definitions byte-identical where "db-migrate exited 0" only reports that a script ran; a function the database lacks is NAMED so an empty result and a healthy one never look alike; and the trap is written into `REPOINT-ARMED` itself -- definitions from hosted are sound because db-migrate keeps schema in step, but DATA IS NOT REPLICATED, so counts, history and "has this ever happened" from hosted are false measurements. THE PART WORTH KEEPING: the first implementation used `grep -Eq` and **the test caught a real bypass I had already written into the working tree** -- grep matches LINE BY LINE, so `crm_capture_lead\nSELECT 1` passes on its first line while carrying a second behind it; `[[ =~ ]]` binds the anchors to the whole string. It surfaced only because the tests EXECUTE the script instead of reading it (DR-0076 independent method), a gate earning its keep on its first day. Proof: 19 new tests, ten of them hostile arguments each running the real script and requiring exit 2 with no network contact; the existing 12-test `sovereign-reader-guard` passes unchanged, so the confidentiality and masking guarantees are untouched; verify green. **DISPATCHED THE SAME DAY, AND IT ANSWERED ON ITS FIRST REAL USE** (run 34739384541, `definitions` with `functions=crm_capture_lead`): the live database returned `source_md5` = `d22ffd311f16111e75f070f1314a51c5`, IDENTICAL to the hosted value, with `---MISSING--- none` and `sovereign_replay=221` -- which closed DR-0374's remaining migration gap by MEASUREMENT rather than by the replay step's word, the entire reason the instrument exists, proven rather than argued. NOT DONE: and only `definitions` mode takes the argument, with the wider "every other proof reads hosted" question left to DR-0368's own re-review rather than duplicated here | accepted | B | infra | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, REVIEW-OUR-WAYS, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, DECISION-RECORDS |
| [DR-0374](DR-0374-the-door-took-orders-the-database-refused-and-nothing-told-anyone.md) | **The door took orders the database refused, and nothing told anyone.** Shay, 2026-09-13, relaying a real customer with a photo of his screen: *"had Ster create an account on the site and he went to send info through email and it won't let him send."* Darrell: *"fix it"* -- and separately, *"does Moore Divahs have a feedback process?"* ROOT CAUSE EXACT: `crm_capture_lead()` allowlists SEVEN pipelines and ends in `ELSE RAISE EXCEPTION`; `crm-engine.js` defines EIGHT; the missing branch is `'moore-orders'`, which is precisely what her door submits. MEASURED rather than assumed -- `crm_leads` holds ZERO rows and the `poe-family` instance DOES exist -- so the finding is not that the form broke but that **it has never once worked since the door shipped**, and Sterling is simply the first customer who told somebody. **0215 IS LIVE ON BOTH DATABASES, THE SOVEREIGN ONE READ BACK DIRECTLY** (hosted: 8 allowlist branches by query; sovereign: `sovereign-read` run 34739384541 returning `source_md5` d22ffd311f16111e75f070f1314a51c5, identical to hosted, which PROVES the definitions byte-identical; deploy run 34738458352 on head_sha 84971bf7). **CORRECTED SAME DAY AGAINST MY OWN EVIDENCE (DR-0076 provenance):** that row count was taken through the Supabase MCP against the HOSTED project, NOT the live database -- `REPOINT-ARMED` is committed, so the app runs on the sovereign NAS stack at `poetech.us/sb` -- and "has never held one" is a claim about history a `count(*)` cannot support at all. The ROOT CAUSE is untouched, because it was never a row-count inference: db-migrate applies to hosted and replays the same checkout onto the sovereign database on the same record, so both carried the identical seven-branch allowlist and both refused `moore-orders` by construction. What the error costs is the CORROBORATION -- zero rows on a mirror is consistent with, not proof of, the live database never taking an order, and this sandbox has no route to measure the live one. Stated rather than smoothed over, and the trap is now written into `REPOINT-ARMED` itself so the next reader meets it before making the same measurement. Why the suite stayed green is the part worth keeping: `moore-door.test.js` exercises the JAVASCRIPT registry and passes, while the database's allowlist is a SECOND registry hand-written in SQL that nothing ever compared -- and 0085, which built this door, says in its own header that it *"Mirrors the crm_capture_lead posture"*; the posture was mirrored and the allowlist was not. Decisions: fix the cause in the database with ONE branch and change nothing else about the function (0215 -- same signature, SECURITY DEFINER, search_path, explicit-only consent, forced-safe insert, tenant pinning; the three values taken from the JS registry, not invented); make the two registries a MACHINE CHECK (`crm-pipeline-parity.test.js`) that parses the migration's CASE block WITH COMMENTS STRIPPED so the prose explaining the fix cannot satisfy the test, requires the sets to match in BOTH directions, pins the per-pipeline business/stage/sequence values, and pins the `ELSE RAISE` wall itself so nobody "fixes" a future mismatch by deleting the allowlist; a failure now SAYS it failed -- *"This did not send -- she has not received it"* replaces *"please try again in a moment"*, which was false twice over (no such moment existed, and the customer was left believing the problem was his timing), with the raw reason going to `console.warn` where a screenshot carries it and never in front of the customer. THE ANSWER TO THE SECOND QUESTION, and it is not the flattering one: **Moore Divahs had NO feedback process** -- `grep -i feedback` over her door returns nothing, and the `'moore'` area registered in `FeedbackCenter` belongs to the main PoeTech app behind our sign-in, not to her storefront -- so the only reason anyone learned Sterling's order vanished is that he told Shay and she told Darrell, which is a favour from a customer rather than a process, and it means the silence lasted exactly as long as the bug. What her door DOES have, further down the same page, is `MyMessages`: a real signed-in customer thread writing to `business_messages`, a different table from the one refusing, **working the whole time eighteen inches below the form that was not, with nothing on screen pointing at it**. The error panel now offers "Message Shay directly instead" and scrolls him there -- which respects the standing `#675` constraint (her email is sign-in only, never rendered) because the route is the in-app thread, not a mailto. The way out is offered ONLY where one exists: the `fallback` prop is optional, so The Practice, which has no thread, keeps the honest generic copy rather than promising a second door that is also missing. Proof: verify green 950 files / 14,183 tests; proven-to-catch TWICE by doctoring the real component -- restoring the old success-regardless behaviour fails 7 of 9, removing only the `fallback` prop fails exactly the 3 about the way out; the parity gate proven against the pre-fix migration; and the failure path tested by MOUNTING the door and submitting the form with the real result shapes from `crm-sync.js`, because "the component renders a failure" is a claim only a mount can settle. NOT DONE: 0215 is not yet executed (read the function's source back after merge -- "the workflow exited 0" and "the branch is live" are two different claims, DR-0371's own lesson); nobody has submitted the form on the live site and the sandbox has no route to poetech.us, so one real inquiry landing as a real row is the confirmation -- re-review 2026-09-20; her door STILL has no general feedback process and neither does any other business door, so the discovery path for the next defect remains "a customer happens to tell Shay" -- re-review 2026-09-27; and NOTHING told us either, since a refused capture raised silently for months with a customer's goodwill as the only witness -- re-review 2026-09-27 | accepted | B | business | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, REALITY-TRACE, APP-IS-PRIMARY, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, DECISION-RECORDS |
| [DR-0373](DR-0373-i-will-not-go-out-free-the-bondservant-answer-to-the-slavery-objection.md) | **I will not go out free: the bondservant answer to the slavery objection.** The friend from L142 returns with a fifth objection — that Yahweh wants us to be His slaves — and laughs. Darrell 2026-09-13: *"I said bond servants who He created and the died for to repurchase because of His Love For Them... lesson."* His sentence IS the lesson, checked against the text. L145 CONCEDES the vocabulary in the first thirty seconds (Paul opens letters as a doulos; denying it loses and deserves to) and frames the error as a DEFINITION error rather than a moral one — the objection reads a nineteenth-century institution back into a document legislating something else. Then the three provisions consecutively, because the force is cumulative: freedom is the default on a clock and FREE (`six years he shall serve: and in the seventh he shall go out free for nothing`); the master must FUND the departure (`Thou shalt furnish him liberally out of thy flock, and out of thy floor, and out of thy winepress`); and permanence requires the SERVANT'S OWN spoken declaration of love, with the law writing his sentence for him (`I love my master, my wife, and my children; I will not go out free`). THE MARK'S LOCATION CARRIES THE ARGUMENT: he is brought `to the door, or unto the door post` and pierced AT THE EXIT HE REFUSED — a record of a refusal to leave, not of a capture. The test is handed to the room rather than the conclusion: no slave code writes automatic release, compulsory severance and a required profession of love into its own statute, because those provisions dissolve the institution. The purchase is INVERTED rather than softened — silver and gold NAMED and refused, the buyer paying with Himself. The standing is then raised in His own mouth with the reason given (disclosure): servant → friend → son → heir. And the premise is denied outright, since `his servants ye are to whom ye obey` means nobody is unowned; the question is only which master, and one pays wages while the other gives a gift. THE CLOSE THE OBJECTION CANNOT FOLLOW: `mine ears hast thou opened` (Psalm 40), which Hebrews places in Christ's mouth entering the world — the Son takes the bondservant's opened ear, the one who stood at the door with the release in His hand and would not go out free. The laugh is answered without flinching and without preaching, ending on one handed-over question rather than a summary (pinned: the lesson text must end on a question mark). Proof: 79 spans all verbatim KJV, with the gate catching FIVE of my own truncations on the first pass (a lowercased Thou, and four quotations closed with a period where the text continues); reading level child 1.9 / teen 6.3 / senior 7.0 / adult 7.4, ascending, no new offenders; 25 tests with each of the three provisions pinned separately and two proven-to-catch. Methodology note kept: the first break attempt replaced only 1 of 6 occurrences and the suite stayed green — an incomplete break, not a weak test, verified before concluding. verify green 948 files / 14,163 tests | accepted | B | word | WORD-FIRST, TEACH-DONT-DEBATE, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, APP-IS-PRIMARY, DECISION-RECORDS |
| [DR-0372](DR-0372-a-false-balance-the-capable-denied-capital-and-the-inheritance-that-needs-no-banks-permission.md) | **A false balance: the capable denied capital, and the inheritance that needs no bank's permission.** Darrell 2026-09-12, continuing L143's economics: *"Money is generated by the individuals who are capable of sustaining the payments"*; refusing ownership to the capable *"limits the growth of your nation"*; *"every older African American man I've spoke to literally said that they had to send a white lady in"*; *"they needed the children to need jobs so they undermine"*; and the close, *"vengeance is still his, but... speaking truth to what is reality is very important to him as well."* L144 is built on the WORD'S OWN CATEGORY rather than on grievance — a false balance is an abomination and `divers weights, a great and a small` are forbidden outright, so two applicants weighed differently is worship, not policy. Ability is established as Heaven's allocation standard first (`to every man according to his several ability`), which makes his opening claim correct underwriting rather than complaint; and the national cost is taken from the text — `that thy days may be lengthened in the land` attached to the honest weight, and `Much food is in the tillage of the poor: but there is that is destroyed for want of judgment`, where the field was productive and outside judgment destroyed the yield. Evidence stated plainly (DR-0100 tier one): Fed SBCS gaps persisting among LOW-CREDIT-RISK firms, NCRC matched-pair testing where BETTER-QUALIFIED Black testers were quoted higher rates and offered less help, and Citigroup's ~$16T lost output of which ~$13T is lost business revenue from discriminatory lending plus ~6.1M jobs never created — that jobs figure kept because it is the mechanism under his own line about children made to need jobs. **THE CORRECTION, and the reason this record exists: he asked for the immigrant-lending claim to be RESEARCHED rather than agreed with** (*"I believe this is a hundred percent true, but we'll see. Go research this"*). The disparity is real and that half stands; the mechanism is generally NOT bank preference but rotating credit associations (kye, hui, susu/esusu), family pooling and community credit unions that BYPASS banking. And the correction is GOOD NEWS: esusu is Yoruba, five centuries old, and crossed the Atlantic in the slave ships — traced in Barbados, Trinidad, South Carolina and New Orleans — so the instrument is an INTERRUPTED INHERITANCE, not another group's advantage; a grievance ends in resentment, a recovered inheritance ends in a storefront. Enforcement removal taught as the same sin in FIGURES: section 1071 narrowed May 2026 to ~31 non-depository institutions (under 2% of that market) by the Bureau's own estimate, EEOC ~2,170 → ~1,809 staff with field attorneys 186 → 159 and merits suits down to 94, DOJ Civil Rights Division down ~three quarters of its attorneys — `To turn aside the needy from judgment` covers closing the court as surely as ruling against him. His own mid-stream refinement (not every company, but in every industry the voices are not heard) is kept OVER his broader first version and pinned. THE TWO WALLS IT SITS BETWEEN, which look like a prohibition until read precisely: 0184 (the giver's own ledger) is owner-only and says an instance admin CANNOT read it, while naming this very surface as the exception — *"a separate, consented, decided surface — never a widening of this policy"* — and 0209 (the pastoral roll) refuses a giving amount BY CONSTRUCTION because *"a church knowing what each person gives is the oldest way a congregation gets quietly sorted."* Both stand untouched: the danger 0209 names is giving appearing on the record a staff member opens while deciding how to treat somebody, so the wall is about WHERE the book may appear, not who may read it — and a church that issues a contribution statement must know who gave what. Decisions: the book is its own tenant-scoped RLS-gated record; read access is `('owner','admin')` with the predicate living ONCE in `church-access-store.js` so the tab, the route and the RLS cannot drift, and NO member policy exists to be widened (member-visible confirmation is its own decided surface, re-review 2026-12-12); the book is never joined to either neighbour and a test enforces it against the CODE with comments stripped, so the prose explaining the boundary survives; THE NAME ON THE STATEMENT IS THE IDENTITY and the account link is optional, because most Cash App givers have no account and an import that could only represent people with logins would drop real money from the church's books; NOTHING is matched automatically at any confidence, since a wrong match puts one member's gift on another's statement, and the database refuses a person-link carrying no confirmer; totals are NEVER forced to agree and an unchecked batch never reads as balanced — the steward enters what her BANK shows because reconciling the file against itself proves nothing; re-import cannot double-count (UNIQUE on the processor's id, in the database not the client); a partial import deletes its own batch and reports, because a wrong book is worse than an empty one. Proof: `npm run verify` green at 944 files / 14,064 tests; 31 tests with TWO proven-to-catch by doctoring the real source and requiring failure (the private-ledger wall, the route's office guard); five repo gates caught real defects — three missing focus rings, not deep-linkable, unreportable in the feedback form — each fixed rather than worked around; monolith freeze held at the documented +1 mount line. VERIFIED SAME DAY, by observation rather than by the workflow's word: merged as `e301091`, `db-migrate` run 34718145238 success, and the DATABASE then read directly because "the workflow exited 0" and "the security property exists" are two different claims — three tables with RLS on, one `<table>_office` policy each (`ALL`/authenticated, owner|admin in both USING and WITH CHECK, NO member clause and no anon grant), both standing overlays applied so an assistant cannot read the congregation's giving, and both integrity walls live in the database rather than only asserted in a test (the confirmer CHECK and the source-ref UNIQUE). Deploy proven per DR-0107 (run 34718145424, head_sha e301091, success). STILL NOT VERIFIED: nobody has opened the screen signed in as the office with a real Cash App export — jsdom is not a browser and a mocked sync layer is not the database; the sandbox has no route to poetech.us (P31) or to the Vercel preview (confirmed: gateway 403 on CONNECT), so that pass belongs on the live build per DR-0104 — re-review 2026-09-19 | accepted | B | church | APP-IS-PRIMARY, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, REALITY-TRACE, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, DECISION-RECORDS |

(3) THE CONTROL GROUP, which is what makes that silence mean something — Peter lifts Cornelius bodily ("I myself also am a man"), Paul and Barnabas REND THEIR CLOTHES (the gesture for blasphemy), and an angel refuses John TWICE in one book. (4) THE VERSE ALMOST NOBODY USES — the SAME John, the SAME book, falls at the feet of the risen Christ and gets no refusal at all, only "Fear not; I am the first and the last": three falls, two refusals, one hand on the shoulder. Corroborated by His enemies ("Hearest thou what these say?" — and He DEFENDED it), Thomas blessed rather than corrected, the Father commanding the angels, and heaven worshipping the Father and the Lamb in one breath. Not trivia, in His own words: "He that honoureth not the Son honoureth not the Father which hath sent him." Closes to the glory of the FATHER, and on who got there first — a leper, a ruler, a mother, a man from the tombs and a man born blind, desperate and right. | [DR-0389](DR-0389-remember-is-a-verb-his-knowing-his-withholding-and-what-the-sea-takes.md) | **Remember is a verb: His knowing, His withholding, and what the sea takes.** Darrell 2026-09-13, asked HONESTLY rather than rhetorically: *"You have to hate Love to actually go to hell... not sure after because of the sea of forgetfulness so we forget those who are lost... however I'm not sure He can block His own knowledge and understanding can he according to the biblical scriptures? I know He can ignore... or not tell you something until He wants to tell you. Word first lesson."* THE FIRST MOVE IS TO SPLIT IT — two questions wearing one coat, answered in OPPOSITE directions. CAN HE STOP KNOWING: the Word never says so and says the reverse ("his understanding is infinite"; "knoweth all things"; "all things are naked and opened unto the eyes of him") and no verse shows Him deleting a fact. CAN HE DECLINE TO TELL: constantly and openly — secret things, a sealed book, thunders not written down, times not for us to know — and He opens on His own clock. SEALED IS NOT DESTROYED, and that second instinct was exactly right. THEN THE VERSE THAT RAISED IT RESOLVES ON A HEBREW HABIT, SHOWN RATHER THAN ASSERTED: remember is an ACTION word, proven from four places where the verb is followed immediately by an act — Noah and the wind in one verse, Abraham and Lot, Hannah, and the thief asking to be ACTED FOR — so "I will remember their sin no more" is A VERDICT, NOT AMNESIA, and stronger for it, because what is forgotten can be recalled and what is judged is finished. AND THE SEA TAKES SINS, NOT PEOPLE — the phrase "sea of forgetfulness" is not in the Word at all; Micah casts SINS in, and every sister promise has the RECORD as its object, never the person. That precision is load-bearing: if the sea swallowed people, heaven would run on managed ignorance and the worry would be fair. TWO DISCIPLINES HELD: the hard verse (Mark 13:32) is READ, stated to stand, and left unexplained rather than smoothed; and where the Word is SILENT the lesson says so — whether the redeemed forget particular PEOPLE is not stated, only the outcome (no more sorrow, no more tears), so no mechanism is supplied, and the test pins that as an ABSENCE because a well-meaning edit would add exactly that comfort. Proof: 147 spans ALL verbatim KJV, the gate catching three places where OUR OWN emphasis and the non-scriptural phrase itself wore quotation marks; zero generic "God"; AND THE READING-LEVEL GATE CAUGHT A REAL DEFECT THAT WAS FIXED RATHER THAN BASELINED — the child band measured 4.0 against a teen 3.2, inverted, so the children's version read HARDER; rewritten to 1.4 and correctly ordered, zero new debt; 31 tests | accepted | B | word | WORD-FIRST, TEACH-DONT-DEBATE, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, SPOKEN-TEACHINGS-ARE-BUILD-INPUT, DECISION-RECORDS |
| [DR-0390](DR-0390-cultural-competency-four-readings-of-one-word-and-the-man-nobody-believed.md) | **Cultural competency: four readings of one word, and the man nobody believed.** Darrell 2026-09-13, across a dozen messages, then naming it himself: *"Lesson!!!!! Cultural Competency Is Important"*. A CORRECTION AS WELL AS AN ADDITION — L146 flattened the word, treating game as purely pejorative, which is one reading of four and is the exact failure this lesson is about. THE THESIS IS HIS OWN SENTENCE AND IT IS THE GUARD, NOT AN ORNAMENT: *"there are different ways to play it but one Way to Win"* — hold both halves or the lesson collapses into one of two failures, a man who cannot hear anyone unlike him, or the fashionable one where every reading is equally true and there is nothing to be saved from. The discipline is about the PLAYING, never the winning, which has one road: "strait is the gate, and narrow is the way", and the Son in the first person. PAUL HOLDS BOTH HALVES ON TRIAL IN ONE SENTENCE — "after the way which they call heresy, so worship I the God of my fathers" — using THEIR word so they know he heard them, and refusing their verdict in the same breath. And in the SAME CHAPTER as the man nobody believed, Saul hunts "any of this way" — the name the believers carried before anyone said Christian. INTEGRITY IS ON THE SAME LIST: "The integrity of the upright shall guide them" — GUIDE, so it is the steering and not the brake — with discretion and understanding as bodyguards, and one verse tying it to the children: "The just man walketh in his integrity: his children are blessed after him." AFTER HIM — the walking IS the inheritance. FOUR READINGS: talk a man cannot back; the game of LIFE (the beating taken, the joy kept — Habakkuk with nothing left saying "Yet I will rejoice in the LORD"); truth told too early and too whole, so its completeness sounds manufactured; and WISDOM — Yahweh wrote the book of it and states its purpose on page one, "To give subtilty to the simple, to the young man knowledge and discretion", naming PRUDENCE as wisdom's housemate. That fourth reading redeems the word and is his own testimony: He gave me the game in Proverbs and Psalms. THE DISCIPLINE is named by the Word, not by us — "folly and shame", a COMPETENCE failure before a love failure — and Paul made the study a POLICY, reading Athens and quoting their own poets, which bought a hearing and not a softer gospel. THE SCENE: Saul tells the truth and is not believed, taught with BOTH SIDES REASONABLE (the suspicion earned by his own past, not by their small hearts); Barnabas supplies EVIDENCE and stakes his name; time delivers the verdict. THE HEART: he knew because HE WAS TOLD — and a man told something by Yahweh speaks with certainty that has NO VISIBLE BASIS, which is the definition of a line, so the very thing that made him sure made him sound false; proving is the only road out, as the Son offered His works. THE ORDER: Him, THEN her — she is ADDED, never the foundation, or she becomes an idol carrying a weight no person was built to carry. THE METHOD for information arriving like game: "Prove all things; hold fast that which is good", then ask, and He "upbraideth not". THE LAST WORD: it is game until you believe Him, then He is Him — Samaria, "Now we believe, not because of thy saying: for we have heard him ourselves". And it reduces to one free instruction: ask Yahweh and read His Word. Proof: 152 spans ALL verbatim KJV, the gate catching three of OUR OWN (the word game itself in quotation marks, and a truncated Paul quote); zero generic "God" against 30 Yahwehs; AND THE READING-LEVEL GATE CAUGHT A REAL INVERSION FIXED RATHER THAN BASELINED — the TEEN band at 6.6 against a senior 5.8, so teenagers read harder than adults; rewritten to 2.3, correctly ordered, zero new debt; 52 tests with THE FOUR READINGS ASSERTED AS A COUNT so an edit dropping the fourth (which arrives last) fails | accepted | B | word | WORD-FIRST, TEACH-DONT-DEBATE, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, SPOKEN-TEACHINGS-ARE-BUILD-INPUT, DECISION-RECORDS |
| [DR-0391](DR-0391-a-list-of-references-is-not-a-sentence-and-a-green-reference-must-open.md) | **A list of references is not a sentence, and a green reference must open.** Darrell 2026-09-13, from the full-screen lesson view with the reader running: *"Links don't work in last played... don't read long list of references together... especially in these.... just when the word or a point is needed"*, then scoping it himself: *"Only lists..."* TWO DEFECTS ON ONE SCREEN, SAME FAMILY. (1) THE READER PERFORMED AN INDEX — an anchor line can carry EIGHTY references separated by semicolons (L149's does, partly this session's own doing), and spoken one at a time that is minutes of "Matthew chapter four verse ten, Isaiah chapter forty-two verse eight..." before a word of teaching, with no way to skip. A LIST performed as prose, the same class as the chrome the reader used to speak. (2) THE ANCHOR'S REFERENCES LOOKED LIKE LINKS AND WERE NOT — plain interpolated text inside a paragraph that is green because the PARAGRAPH is green, advertising the "tap any verse reference" affordance the rest of the app keeps. The hollow-surface class (DR-0381) in a different coat. DECISIONS: the rule is about RUNS, never about references — exactly his "Only lists" line — so three or more ADJACENT references collapse to one short sentence naming the count and where they are, while one or two stay spoken in full and prose between two references protects both, because a citation in a sentence IS the point; it never silently drops content; it collapses BEFORE expansion and AFTER un-shouting (order load-bearing twice); the matcher stays in the one shared scanner; and the anchor line is wired to WordInline so every reference opens the verbatim KJV in place. PROVEN-TO-CATCH IN BOTH DIRECTIONS, because this rule can fail two opposite ways: disable the collapse → 3 tests fail; make it GREEDY (min=1) → 5 fail, every one protecting a citation in prose — a fix that over-applied would have destroyed the thing he asked to keep; revert the anchor to plain text → the wiring test fails. LIMIT STATED: "links don't work in last played" had two readings and one was acted on (the play view's anchor, where a real defect was confirmed in the code); the Recently-opened chips call the same open(id) the main list uses and were left alone — re-review 2026-09-20 | accepted | B | orchestration | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, COMPREHENSIVE-REVIEW-STANDARD, APP-IS-PRIMARY, DECISION-RECORDS |

| [DR-0392](DR-0392-recently-opened-is-a-link-play-starts-the-lesson-and-the-reference-list-moves-to-the-end.md) | **Recently Opened is a link, Play starts the lesson, and the reference list moves to the end.** Darrell 2026-09-13, from the Learn tab and one real slide: *"Recently Opened should be links like the others so users can click where they were!!!!!!! Also the last lessons dont click when you click them"*, *"Let the Play buttons just start the lessons and the PowerPoints have too many references"*, and *"Put the list of links at the end of lessons for reference purposes... so it doesn't take away from the lessons."* FIVE FINDINGS, AND THE NASTIEST WAS INVISIBLE: the arrival effect was keyed on `resumeLessonId`, so setting that state to the SAME id is a React no-op — the render is skipped, the effect never runs, the tap does nothing, with no error and no half-open state. Nearly invisible on the main list (you rarely re-tap the lesson you just left) and THE NORMAL CASE on "Recently opened", which is BY DEFINITION the lessons you already opened — so the most likely tap on that row was the one guaranteed to do nothing; a rising nonce makes arrival an EVENT rather than a VALUE. The chip became a LINK because a row whose whole purpose is "return to where you were" must look like the thing you return with. Play landed on the presenter CONSOLE — a setup screen with its own START button — so the control named Play played nothing until a second tap; it now arrives already presenting. AND THE CAP IS A MOVE, NOT A DELETION, which is the whole decision: a lesson's anchor carries every reference the WHOLE lesson stands on (L149's is eighty) and the slide printed the entire string — a LIST rendered as content, DR-0391's defect in a different medium — so teaching slides name only the few the session opens on with an honest "+77 more" and the closing "The Word we stood on" slide carries the whole list, while IN-LINE references throughout the lesson are untouched, exactly as he asked. THE CAP NEEDED SOMEWHERE TO LAND: six lessons author no run-of-show and so never had a closing slide, and capping their one teaching slide would have dropped the list out of the deck ALTOGETHER rather than moving it to the end — caught by the corpus-wide test, not by reading. A TEST WAS RE-POINTED ON PURPOSE, NOT WEAKENED: the Play assertion was not wrong when written, it described the behaviour he asked to change, so it now pins the NEW contract rather than being deleted. Proven-to-catch: drop the nonce from the effect deps, or stop bumping it in open(), and the re-tap test fails; 24 new tests; 971 files, 14,595 tests green | accepted | B | orchestration | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, APP-IS-PRIMARY, COMPREHENSIVE-REVIEW-STANDARD, DECISION-RECORDS |

Decisions: (1) lessonSections groups a section's lines into blocks of ≤6 lines, closing early rather than exceed 9 chips per strip (SECTION_RHYTHM), one strip per block at its foot — not one word moves, only where the green waits; each strip stamps data-block-lines. (2) The lesson-space bar and the catalog lessons-bar take .ts-chrome-region (DR-0276's one cap); the reading pill/panel buttons drop their rem floor for em; the text-size panel chips fix their box in px like their labels. (3) The chrome-layout probe's lesson pass asserts the rhythm from the DOM and loads the lesson at Big Print to assert the bar's buttons and the chips stay under 64px; selftest must trip five ways. Measured in real Chromium: selftest 11 tripped (5 lesson); sweep 33/33 + 4/4 lesson + 16 text-scale; L1 now 4 strips, max 2 chips, ≤6 lines/block; at Big Print the bar's buttons 47px and chips 46px (un-capped ~100px / 132px). Ten new tests incl. every catalog lesson within the rhythm and L128's wall proven gone | accepted | B | ux | REALITY-TRACE, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, APP-IS-PRIMARY, DECISION-RECORDS |

Both were real mechanisms. The place record stored where the reader last was and nothing else, so the final sentence spoken was the sentence saved, and the next press of Read this lesson resolved it, spoke one line, and stopped; nothing in the chain could tell a pause from a finish. The record now carries `done`, marked when the last sentence of the piece is spoken and cleared the moment any reading moves again, so a finished lesson starts at the top while an interrupted re-listen still resumes. The lesson space opens a finished lesson at part one. The bar's way-out became the bar's primary control (filled, 44px, and it says All lessons on a phone), and the end of every lesson now carries two full-width doors, Start over and All lessons, where a disabled Done was the only thing there. Proven to catch four ways: the same saved sentence one field apart reads the tail or the whole lesson, and removing the short-circuit, the end-mark, the primary bar button, or the end doors each turns the new gate red. | [DR-0453](DR-0453-the-projector-follows-the-one-dial-the-rooms-text-size-reaches-the-second-display.md) | **The projector follows the one dial: the room's text size reaches the second display.** DR-0451 gave the room's text size a control on the presenting screen and named, in its own Limits, the thing it had not done: the second display kept the default, which is backwards for the one case the feature exists for, because a phone held up is a stopgap and a projector across a hall is the real room. Closed now rather than at its dated re-review. The projector gets no control of its own, since nobody stands at it during a service; the size rides the slide it is already following over the same channel, so one dial moves both screens. Three properties keep it safe: a payload without a scale renders at 1, so an older presenter or a hold behaves exactly as before; a size change re-sends at once, because a speaker enlarges the words precisely when the back row cannot read this slide; and blanking the screen holds the last size, since the choice belongs to the session and not to one slide. The value is read through a ref so changing it never re-creates the broadcast callback. Proven to catch four ways; full suite 15,669 passed. Verified at the payload layer, not on two real displays, and that limit is stated with a date. | accepted | A | app | VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, NOTHING-WAITS, APP-IS-PRIMARY, DECISION-RECORDS |

| [DR-0446](DR-0446-the-church-doors-offline-shell-is-ported-onto-main-rather-than-waiting-for-a-stale-branch.md) | **The church door's offline shell is ported onto main rather than waiting for a stale branch.** DR-0444 dated the per-door offline fallback 2026-10-07 and called it "real but narrow"; both halves were wrong. For a /lovecorner/app/ navigation the BASE-bound fallback resolves to the wrong app's shell, or to `undefined` — and respondWith(undefined) IS a network error, which Chrome renders as ERR_FAILED on one 4G blip. (1) `LessonProse` rendered every paragraph as plain `<p>{it.text}</p>`, so a sentence naming "Lamentations 3:22-23" gave the reader nothing to press — while `WordInline` had existed since DR-0340/0341 (2026-09-08) and EternalAlgorithmsStudy had used it on prose for weeks. He is right that the solution was already ours; the lesson body, the highest-traffic prose in the app, was the one surface never wired to it. (2) The Open block printed `anchor.ref` verbatim — every reference the WHOLE lesson stands on, 41 for L151 — a bibliography occupying a teaching slot. (3) Two blocks nobody asked for, both added by the agent that same day: "Questions to sit with" (13:48, 3c6271a2) and "The Word we stood on" (02:32, a56781ac), which together are why SEND-OFF STOPPED BEING THE FINAL PAGE. DECISIONS: the reference opens where the sentence names it (`LessonProse` → `WordInline`; prose unchanged to the character, the chip shows the author's own words); `WordInline` gained a `prefix` slot and `data-*`/`tabIndex` pass-through, load-bearing because a naive swap would have silently dropped the numbered-section badge, the speaker index's `data-point-index` scroll target, the paragraph stepper's `data-para-index` and the focus target — the same trade-one-working-surface-for-another class that bit three times this session; `anchorIsRun()` is true at 3+, reusing `referencesIn` and the SAME threshold the voice already uses (DR-0391 D1 — the rule is about RUNS, never references), so 1–2 still print with their point and a run yields to the theme, losing nothing because every reference recurs in the prose that discusses it; Send-off is last again. AND THE WAYS: audited all 13 merges of 2026-09-14 — ten carry a record, THREE DO NOT (#1578 18:32, the change that put the chip wall on his lessons; #1579 L151; #1580 the revert), all Tier-B on the lesson surface inside two hours, no `hold`, no Ways review. The cause was ALREADY WRITTEN DOWN — this INDEX's 2026-09-13 ledger-drift finding named it verbatim ("a run of fast merges in one session with no gate requiring a DR for a Tier-B change") — and recurred the next day, which is the proof that prose is not a safeguard (DR-0250). So `scripts/decision-record-guard.mjs` REFUSES `npm run ship` when a branch changes app/src, infra or scripts and adds no docs/decisions file; it rides `ship` not CI for DR-0393's reason (CI's base may be shallow, and "did this session write down why" belongs where the author still stands); UNKNOWN exits 2, never green. Suite green: 990 files / 14,812 tests. LIMITS, STATED: the guard checks a record EXISTS, not that it is true or the tier right — it closes the silence, not the judgment; `ship` only, no hooks; the threshold is one constant if 2 turns out righter; the in-sentence opening is the one thing ADDED rather than restored (asked for twice in his own words, one line at two call sites to hold); and the per-section "4 scriptures with a button up top" from his Prophets screenshot is NOT built — `ShowTheWordToggle` is app-wide today — named rather than dropped — re-review 2026-09-21 | accepted | B | orchestration | APP-IS-PRIMARY, VERIFICATION-DOCTRINE, MACHINERY-OVER-MEMORY, REALITY-TRACE, PERPETUAL-IMPROVEMENT, DECISION-RECORDS |

Three measured facts: production configures NEITHER endpoint (the deploy build sets no VITE_VOICE_SERVICE_URL and no VITE_VOICE_BRIDGE, no .env files, so activeVoiceEndpoint() is null and line 281 never fires on the live site); the studio is in NO manifest and has NO installer — precisely DR-0330's first cause for the /taxes hop; AND THE DECISIVE ONE, no server could serve a built-in voice AT ALL — server.py returned 400 reference-required the moment no sample arrived, and the vendor bridge does the same, so BOTH endpoints were clone-only by construction. DR-0382's careful runtime probe was therefore guaranteed to learn 'no' on its first request, FOREVER: the client was taught to ask, no server was ever taught to answer. AND THE CLAIM CARRIED FOR TWO MONTHS: DR-0382 recorded "the probe is unanswered until a real read happens on a real device" — never true, because THE REPO IS THE DEPLOYMENT and reading our own server.py answered it in under a minute; meanwhile gap-voice-clone's re-review sat 52 DAYS OVERDUE with nothing surfacing it. The sharp lesson: the honest-sounding hedge ("we cannot know until a real device tries") READ AS DILIGENCE AND FUNCTIONED AS A BLOCKER, deferring a check that cost one file read. DECISIONS: the studio serves its own speaker bank when no sample arrives; HONEST FAILURE IS PRESERVED DELIBERATELY (a model with no speaker bank still returns 400, so the probe's 'no' stays TRUE — papering that over would be worse than the original bug); the clone path is untouched; the bridge stays clone-only, correctly. Everything DR-0334/DR-0336 shipped is real and live and was never reached, because the ONLY control that can subscribe a device renders in exactly one place -- part-way down the Messages tab -- and nothing else in the app ever said notifications were off. The off-state was never false; it was never SAID. Second defect, same root: `notifyDecision` rightly never rings while the app is on screen, and the app then showed NOTHING either (no title bar inside an installed PWA, the launcher badge behind the app you are in, the unread count only on Messages). DECISION: three states told apart (`unsupported`/`unconfigured`/`blocked`/`on`/`off`, and permission is NOT a subscription); an app-wide `AppAlerts` layer in its own root (the shell is frozen at 5355) that HOSTS the existing control rather than reimplementing it, so permission is still only ever asked from a press; an in-app arrival pill that is the exact COMPLEMENT of the doorbell -- no overlap, no gap -- landing in the door the person already stands in (`messageLandingHere`); one DM subscription in the page feeding every listener via `poetech:dm-unread`; and "not now" that expires after 14 days so it never becomes "never". 38 checks, **22/22 breaks caught, 0 theatre** -- and the ONE break missed on the first pass is the session's recurring finding in its purest form: commenting out `startDmNotifications(window)` left the source regex green, because a check that proves a line is PRESENT proves nothing about whether it EXECUTES (every wiring check now reads comment-stripped source). A GATE WAS WRONG AND WAS CORRECTED RATHER THAN WORKED AROUND: `consistency-guard` refused the new layer on `width-cap 2 > 0`, but DR-0246's own fix text says its target is the TAB WRAPPER and that modals live inside the full-width container -- and `PwaPrompts`' two existing banners are grandfathered for exactly this shape, the tell that the COUNT was wrong, not the code. A `max-w-` is now uncounted only when its own class span also carries `fixed` (comment lines stripped, as `fixedPx` already did); narrow per class span, so a tab wrapper one line away still counts and a cap outside a quoted span still counts. Measured: 11 caps across 9 files no longer counted, all already below their frozen baseline, no file's count rose; 6 checks hold it, one against the real file asserting its caps are present before asserting zero. Still NOT proven: no real phone has yet received a real notification -- what closes here is the reason it was impossible (`re-review: 2026-09-24`). |
| [DR-0456](DR-0456-i-always-had-love-a-coworkers-answer-became-a-measure-and-two-words-the-verse-does-not-carry.md) | **I always had love: a coworker's answer became a measure, and two words the verse does not carry.** Darrell 2026-09-17, spoken: a coworker told him he does not really have contact with his family, and he answered with his own count -- thirteen uncles and aunts, an interesting life, and love the whole way -- then *"you suffer with me and you reign with me"*, *"he don't give you the spirit of fear, but a love, peace, joy, and sound mind"*, and *"get down with the team, stay with the king, let's get home."* DECISION: L165 takes his clauses in the order he said them -- gratitude as a count made on purpose and then refused the comfort of being un-seen (Psalms 103:2), the hand that sets the solitary in families naming the coworker in the same breath (Psalms 68:6), love as the greatest of the three that abide (1 Corinthians 13:13), the order nothing reverses taught as INHERITANCE and not wage (2 Timothy 2:11-12; Romans 8:17), joy as strength for the middle rather than a wage at the end (Nehemiah 8:10), the hazard Ecclesiastes actually names being falling ALONE (Ecclesiastes 4:10), and an ending that gives the man with no contact a SEAT and not sympathy (Psalms 27:10; Matthew 12:50). THE NEAR-MISS, TAUGHT OUT LOUD: he named five things the Spirit gives, and **2 Timothy 1:7 carries three of them and does NOT carry peace or joy** -- the easy invisible failure was to quote the verse with five words in it, which nobody would have caught, so every band instead teaches the handling and fetches Galatians 5:22-23. Two places, five words, one Spirit; his word is fully in the Word and the quotation is simply not edited to prove it. Measured: adult prose 1,334 words, bands at 0.637 / 0.770 / 0.860 / 1.060 against floors of 0.50 / 0.60 / 0.60 / 0.60; reading ladder 1.3 / 3.3 / 6.0 / 10.4, monotone, child under the 5.0 new-lesson ceiling; 164 quoted spans, 164 verbatim under a STRICT comparison, 0 unreferenced. The pre-authoring batch caught two alterations before a word was written: a comma inserted into Proverbs 4:7 and a lower-cased *and* opening Romans 8:17. A THIRD got past it, and the batch itself was the reason: it normalised U+2019 to an ASCII apostrophe IN BOTH DIRECTIONS, so nine spans across four bands carried ASCII apostrophes where the corpus carries typographic ones (Galatians 6:2, 1 Peter 4:13). The catalog-wide apostrophe gate caught them all; the L165 gate could not see them either, so its comparison is now whitespace-only -- a check that forgives the very character it is checking is not a check. 21 checks, **20/20 breaks caught, 0 theatre** -- and the ONE missed on the first pass is this pass's recurring finding in new clothes: the order-is-fixed check accepted *before reigning* anywhere in the band and was held green by the band's own OPENING LINE, the title restated to satisfy the title-in-narrative rule, so deleting every sentence that fixed the order left the gate green. Title words are now uniquely dangerous to match on. All three shrink-only baselines changed by exactly one line (the count); no debt entry added. Limits: the coworker is a real person known from one relayed sentence, so the lesson does not characterise him or assume a cause. |

Humble yourself under the mighty hand of the Lord."* DECISION: L166 is L6's COMPLEMENT, not its repeat -- L6 teaches the Philippians 4:8 filter, this teaches the REASON most teaching omits, walked through the text rather than asserted over it (Proverbs 23:7; Jeremiah 17:9; Matthew 12:34-35): disrespect in, nothing filtering it, it becomes what you think, what you think becomes what you are, and it comes out on somebody who had no part in it -- not a monster, just you unfiltered after enough weather. Self defined as he defined it and DENIED rather than improved, daily (Galatians 5:16-17; Romans 8:6; Luke 9:23). Servant AND king held together or neither is true, with both failures named -- a king who will not serve is a tyrant, a servant who forgets he is a king gets used (Revelation 1:6; 5:10; 1 Peter 2:9; Mark 10:43-44; Matthew 20:28; John 13:14). | [DR-0458](DR-0458-the-merge-landed-24-seconds-after-the-poll-gave-up-so-the-healer-hears-the-merge-instead.md) | **The merge landed 24 seconds after the poll gave up, so the healer hears the merge instead.** MEASURED while confirming the deploy for #1648 per DR-0107: the PR merged at 18:21:12Z, auto-merge's heal-deploy poll ran 18:07:53 -> 18:20:48 and gave up, and NO deploy fired for main's tip 7d318655. ADDITIVE ON PURPOSE: the cron, the manual dispatch and auto-merge's own heal-deploy all stay, so the cost of being wrong is today's behaviour and nothing worse, and the gate breaks if any of the three is removed. Three brakes kept: bounded looks (the gate fails if tries x gap exceeds five minutes), the existing single-instance lock, the existing DEPLOY_FRESHNESS_ENABLED kill-switch. 9 checks, **18/18 breaks caught, 0 theatre** -- and the one that earns its keep is CROSS-FILE: a workflow_run trigger matches the upstream workflow's `name:`, so renaming Auto-merge is a one-word edit in another file that silently unhooks the healer, and the gate reads both files and compares. NOT PROVEN: that a workflow_run on Auto-merge reaches this workflow at all -- the suppression the header describes is of events EMITTED BY a GITHUB_TOKEN action, and a run's completion is emitted by GitHub about its own run, but that is a reading, not a measurement (`re-review: 2026-09-18`, one merge away, with the fallback named). SECOND FINDING, tracked separately and NOT fixed here: every `pull_request`-triggered run of auto-merge.yml produces ZERO jobs and ends `failure` or `action_required` (runs 4371, 4373, 4375, 4378), i.e. it waits for an approval it never gets, so half the lane is dead -- including the `labeled` path the DR-0103 `hold` brake rides, which means the Governor's hold may not disarm an armed PR.
